import os
import io
import csv
from datetime import datetime, date, timedelta
from functools import wraps
from flask import (Flask, render_template_string, request, redirect,
                   url_for, flash, session, send_from_directory, Response,
                   jsonify)
from flask_sqlalchemy import SQLAlchemy
from werkzeug.security import generate_password_hash, check_password_hash
from werkzeug.utils import secure_filename
from sqlalchemy import func, or_, and_, extract

# ---------------- الإعدادات ----------------
BASE_DIR = os.path.abspath(os.path.dirname(__file__))
UPLOAD_FOLDER = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'med_uploads_v2')
STATIC_FOLDER = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'med_static_v2')
BACKUP_FOLDER = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'med_backup_v2')
os.makedirs(UPLOAD_FOLDER, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(BACKUP_FOLDER, exist_ok=True)

app = Flask(__name__)
app.config['SECRET_KEY'] = 'med-tech-pro-v2-2026-final-key'
app.config['SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI'] = 'sqlite:///' + os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'med_tech_v2.db')
app.config['SQLALCHEMY_TRACK_MODIFICATIONS'] = False
app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'] = UPLOAD_FOLDER
app.config['BACKUP_FOLDER'] = BACKUP_FOLDER
app.config['MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH'] = 32 * 1024 * 1024

db = SQLAlchemy(app)


# ============================================================
#                     النماذج (Models)
# ============================================================

class User(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    username = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password = db.Column(db.String(200), nullable=False)
    full_name = db.Column(db.String(100))
    role = db.Column(db.String(30), default='engineer')
    email = db.Column(db.String(100))
    phone = db.Column(db.String(20))
    active = db.Column(db.Boolean, default=True)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)


class Department(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(100), nullable=False)
    floor = db.Column(db.String(20))
    building = db.Column(db.String(50))
    manager_name = db.Column(db.String(100))
    manager_phone = db.Column(db.String(20))
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)


class Equipment(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    code = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(150), nullable=False)
    category = db.Column(db.String(50))
    manufacturer = db.Column(db.String(100))
    model = db.Column(db.String(100))
    serial_number = db.Column(db.String(100))
    department_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('department.id'))
    location = db.Column(db.String(150))
    purchase_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    install_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    warranty_end = db.Column(db.Date)
    price = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    supplier = db.Column(db.String(150))
    status = db.Column(db.String(30), default='يعمل')
    criticality = db.Column(db.String(20), default='عادي')
    last_maintenance = db.Column(db.Date)
    next_maintenance = db.Column(db.Date)
    maintenance_period_days = db.Column(db.Integer, default=180)
    image = db.Column(db.String(200))
    manual_file = db.Column(db.String(200))
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    department = db.relationship('Department', foreign_keys=[department_id])


class Engineer(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('user.id'))
    name = db.Column(db.String(100), nullable=False)
    employee_number = db.Column(db.String(50))
    job_title = db.Column(db.String(50))
    specialty = db.Column(db.String(100))
    license_number = db.Column(db.String(50))
    phone = db.Column(db.String(20))
    email = db.Column(db.String(100))
    hire_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    salary = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    active = db.Column(db.Boolean, default=True)
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    user = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[user_id])


class TrainingRecord(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    engineer_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('engineer.id'))
    course_name = db.Column(db.String(200), nullable=False)
    provider = db.Column(db.String(150))
    start_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    end_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    hours = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    certificate_number = db.Column(db.String(100))
    cost = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    status = db.Column(db.String(20), default='مكتمل')
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    engineer = db.relationship('Engineer', foreign_keys=[engineer_id])


class PerformanceReview(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    engineer_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('engineer.id'))
    review_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    period = db.Column(db.String(50))
    quality_score = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    speed_score = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    teamwork_score = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    knowledge_score = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    total_score = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    rating = db.Column(db.String(20))
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    engineer = db.relationship('Engineer', foreign_keys=[engineer_id])


class MaintenanceRecord(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    number = db.Column(db.String(30), unique=True)
    equipment_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('equipment.id'))
    type = db.Column(db.String(30))
    description = db.Column(db.Text)
    reported_by = db.Column(db.String(100))
    assigned_to = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('engineer.id'))
    status = db.Column(db.String(20), default='جديد')
    priority = db.Column(db.String(20), default='عادي')
    start_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    end_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    duration_hours = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    cost = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    parts_used = db.Column(db.Text)
    result = db.Column(db.Text)
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    equipment = db.relationship('Equipment', foreign_keys=[equipment_id])
    engineer = db.relationship('Engineer', foreign_keys=[assigned_to])


class MaintenanceImage(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    maintenance_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('maintenance_record.id'))
    stage = db.Column(db.String(20))
    filename = db.Column(db.String(200))
    uploaded_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)


class MaintenancePlan(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    equipment_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('equipment.id'))
    plan_name = db.Column(db.String(150))
    period_days = db.Column(db.Integer, default=180)
    last_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    next_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    estimated_hours = db.Column(db.Float, default=1)
    checklist = db.Column(db.Text)
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    active = db.Column(db.Boolean, default=True)
    equipment = db.relationship('Equipment', foreign_keys=[equipment_id])


class Ticket(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    number = db.Column(db.String(30), unique=True)
    title = db.Column(db.String(200), nullable=False)
    equipment_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('equipment.id'))
    reported_by = db.Column(db.String(100))
    department_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('department.id'))
    description = db.Column(db.Text)
    priority = db.Column(db.String(20), default='عادي')
    status = db.Column(db.String(20), default='مفتوح')
    assigned_to = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('engineer.id'))
    opened_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    closed_at = db.Column(db.DateTime)
    response_hours = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    resolution_hours = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    equipment = db.relationship('Equipment', foreign_keys=[equipment_id])
    department = db.relationship('Department', foreign_keys=[department_id])
    engineer = db.relationship('Engineer', foreign_keys=[assigned_to])


class ChecklistTemplate(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(150), nullable=False)
    category = db.Column(db.String(50))

    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)


class Calibration(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    number = db.Column(db.String(30), unique=True)
    equipment_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('equipment.id'))
    calibration_date = db.Column(db.Date)
    next_calibration = db.Column(db.Date)
    result = db.Column(db.String(30))
    certificate_number = db.Column(db.String(100))
    calibrated_by = db.Column(db.String(100))
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    equipment = db.relationship('Equipment', foreign_keys=[equipment_id])


class SparePart(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    code = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(150), nullable=False)
    category = db.Column(db.String(50))
    compatible_with = db.Column(db.String(200))
    manufacturer = db.Column(db.String(100))
    supplier = db.Column(db.String(150))
    unit = db.Column(db.String(20), default='قطعة')
    purchase_price = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    quantity = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    alert_limit = db.Column(db.Float, default=5)
    location = db.Column(db.String(100))
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)


class PartUsage(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    maintenance_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('maintenance_record.id'))
    part_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('spare_part.id'))
    quantity = db.Column(db.Float, default=1)
    unit_price = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    total = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    maintenance = db.relationship('MaintenanceRecord', foreign_keys=[maintenance_id])
    part = db.relationship('SparePart', foreign_keys=[part_id])


class Supplier(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    name = db.Column(db.String(150), nullable=False)
    contact_person = db.Column(db.String(100))
    phone = db.Column(db.String(20))
    phone2 = db.Column(db.String(20))
    email = db.Column(db.String(100))
    address = db.Column(db.String(200))
    category = db.Column(db.String(50))
    rating = db.Column(db.Integer, default=3)
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)


class SupplierContact(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    supplier_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('supplier.id'))
    contact_date = db.Column(db.Date, default=date.today)
    subject = db.Column(db.String(200))
    details = db.Column(db.Text)
    contact_type = db.Column(db.String(30))
    follow_up = db.Column(db.Date)
    created_at = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    supplier = db.relationship('Supplier', foreign_keys=[supplier_id])


class Budget(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    year = db.Column(db.Integer, nullable=False)
    category = db.Column(db.String(100))
    description = db.Column(db.String(200))

    spent = db.Column(db.Float, default=0)
    notes = db.Column(db.Text)

class ActivityLog(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    user_id = db.Column(db.Integer, db.ForeignKey('user.id'))
    action = db.Column(db.String(100))
    details = db.Column(db.Text)
    ip = db.Column(db.String(50))
    date = db.Column(db.DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    user = db.relationship('User', foreign_keys=[user_id])


class Setting(db.Model):
    id = db.Column(db.Integer, primary_key=True)
    key = db.Column(db.String(50), unique=True)
    value = db.Column(db.String(500))


# ============================================================
#                     مساعدات عامة
# ============================================================

def get_setting(key, default=''):
    s = Setting.query.filter_by(key=key).first()
    return s.value if s else default


def set_setting(key, value):
    s = Setting.query.filter_by(key=key).first()
    if s:
        s.value = str(value)
    else:
        db.session.add(Setting(key=key, value=str(value)))
    db.session.commit()


def login_required(f):
    @wraps(f)
    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
        if not session.get('user_id'):
            return redirect(url_for('login'))
        return f(*args, **kwargs)
    return wrapper


def admin_required(f):
    @wraps(f)
    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
        u = current_user()
        if not u or u.role not in ('admin', 'head'):
            flash('⛔ غير مصرح لك بالدخول', 'danger')
            return redirect(url_for('dashboard'))
        return f(*args, **kwargs)
    return wrapper


def head_required(f):
    @wraps(f)
    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
        u = current_user()
        if not u or u.role not in ('admin', 'head'):
            flash('⛔ هذا القسم لرئيس القسم فقط', 'danger')
            return redirect(url_for('dashboard'))
        return f(*args, **kwargs)
    return wrapper


def next_number(model, prefix):
    # توليد رقم تسلسلي يعيد استخدام الأرقام المحذوفة
    try:
        records = model.query.all()
        existing_nums = set()
        for r in records:
            if hasattr(r, 'number') and r.number:
                try:
                    existing_nums.add(int(r.number.split('-')[-1]))
                except (ValueError, IndexError):
                    pass
            if hasattr(r, 'code') and r.code:
                try:
                    existing_nums.add(int(r.code.split('-')[-1]))
                except (ValueError, IndexError):
                    pass
        n = 1
        while n in existing_nums:
            n += 1
        return f"{prefix}-{n:06d}"
    except Exception:
        last = model.query.order_by(model.id.desc()).first()
        n = (last.id + 1) if last else 1
        return f"{prefix}-{n:06d}"


def next_id_available(model):
    # إرجاع أصغر ID غير مستخدم
    try:
        records = model.query.all()
        existing_ids = set(r.id for r in records)
        n = 1
        while n in existing_ids:
            n += 1
        return n
    except Exception:
        return 1

def current_user():
    uid = session.get('user_id')
    return User.query.get(uid) if uid else None


def log_activity(action, details=''):
    try:
        u = current_user()
        if u:
            db.session.add(ActivityLog(
                user_id=u.id, action=action, details=details,
                ip=request.remote_addr or ''
            ))
            db.session.commit()
    except Exception:
        db.session.rollback()


def get_alerts_count():
    try:
        today = date.today()
        future_30 = date.fromordinal(today.toordinal() + 30)
        count = 0
        count += Equipment.query.filter(
            Equipment.next_maintenance <= future_30,
            Equipment.next_maintenance >= today,
            Equipment.status == 'يعمل'
        ).count()
        count += Equipment.query.filter_by(status='معطل').count()
        count += Equipment.query.filter(
            Equipment.next_maintenance < today,
            Equipment.status == 'يعمل'
        ).count()
        count += SparePart.query.filter(
            SparePart.quantity <= SparePart.alert_limit
        ).count()
        count += Ticket.query.filter_by(status='مفتوح').count()
        return count
    except Exception:
        return 0


def get_current_month_range():
    today = date.today()
    start = today.replace(day=1)
    if today.month == 12:
        end = today.replace(year=today.year + 1, month=1, day=1) - timedelta(days=1)
    else:
        end = today.replace(month=today.month + 1, day=1) - timedelta(days=1)
    return start, end


def get_today():
    return date.today()


app.jinja_env.globals['current_user'] = current_user
app.jinja_env.globals['get_setting'] = get_setting
app.jinja_env.globals['get_alerts_count'] = get_alerts_count
app.jinja_env.globals['today'] = get_today
app.jinja_env.globals['timedelta'] = timedelta
app.jinja_env.globals['date'] = date
app.jinja_env.globals['now'] = datetime.now

<script src="{{ url_for('med_static_v2', filename='js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js') }}"></script>
<script>
function toggleTheme() {
  document.body.classList.toggle('dark-mode');
  const isDark = document.body.classList.contains('dark-mode');
  localStorage.setItem('theme', isDark ? 'dark' : 'light');
  document.querySelector('.theme-toggle').textContent = isDark ? '☀️' : '🌙';
}

function playNotification() {
  try {
    const ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(ctx.destination);
    osc.frequency.value = 700; osc.type = 'sine';
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, ctx.currentTime);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.5);
    osc.start(ctx.currentTime); osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.5);
  } catch(e) {}
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  if (localStorage.getItem('theme') === 'dark') {
    document.body.classList.add('dark-mode');
    const btn = document.querySelector('.theme-toggle');
    if (btn) btn.textContent = '☀️';
  }
  {% if session.get('user_id') %}
    const alertCount = {{ get_alerts_count() }};
    if (alertCount > 0 && !sessionStorage.getItem('alertPlayed')) {
      setTimeout(playNotification, 1500);
      sessionStorage.setItem('alertPlayed', '1');
    }
  {% endif %}
});
</script>
{% block scripts %}{% endblock %}
</body>
</html>
"""


def render(template, **kwargs):
    full = BASE_TEMPLATE.replace(
        '{% block content %}{% endblock %}',
        '{% block content %}' + template + '{% endblock %}'
    )
    return render_template_string(full, **kwargs)


@app.route('/med_static_v2/<path:filename>')
def med_static_v2(filename):
    return send_from_directory(STATIC_FOLDER, filename)


# ============================================================
#                     المصادقة
# ============================================================

@app.route('/login', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def login():
    if session.get('user_id'):
        return redirect(url_for('dashboard'))
    if request.method == 'POST':
        u = User.query.filter_by(username=request.form['username']).first()
        if u and u.active and check_password_hash(u.password, request.form['password']):
            session['user_id'] = u.id
            session['role'] = u.role
            log_activity('login', f'تسجيل دخول: {u.username}')
            flash('✅ مرحباً بك في MED-TECH PRO v2.1', 'success')
            return redirect(url_for('dashboard'))
        flash('❌ بيانات الدخول خاطئة', 'danger')
    return render('''
    <div class="row justify-content-center" style="margin-top:60px;">
      <div class="col-md-4">
        <div class="card p-4">
          <div class="text-center mb-4">
            <div style="font-size:56px; color:#1565C0;">🏥</div>
            <h3 style="color:#1565C0; font-weight:bold;">MED-TECH PRO</h3>
            <p style="color:#78909C; letter-spacing:3px; font-size:11px; margin:0;">VERSION 2.1</p>
            <hr>
            <p style="color:#90A4AE; font-size:12px;">نظام إدارة هندسة الأجهزة الطبية</p>
          </div>
          <form method="post">
            <div class="mb-3"><label>اسم المستخدم</label>
              <input name="username" class="form-control form-control-lg" required autofocus
                     placeholder="admin"></div>
            <div class="mb-3"><label>كلمة المرور</label>
              <input name="password" type="password" class="form-control form-control-lg" required
                     placeholder="••••••••"></div>
            <button class="btn btn-primary w-100 btn-lg">🔐 دخول</button>
          </form>
          <hr>
          <small class="text-muted text-center d-block">
            افتراضي: <b>admin / admin123</b>
          </small>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    ''')


@app.route('/logout')
def logout():
    log_activity('logout', 'تسجيل خروج')
    session.clear()
    return redirect(url_for('login'))


# ============================================================
#                     معالج الأخطاء
# ============================================================

@app.errorhandler(404)
def not_found(e):
    return render('''
    <div class="text-center" style="padding:80px;">
      <div style="font-size:100px;">🔍</div>
      <h2>الصفحة غير موجودة</h2>
      <a href="{{ url_for('dashboard') }}" class="btn btn-primary">← لوحة التحكم</a>
    </div>'''), 404


@app.errorhandler(500)
def server_error(e):
    return render('''
    <div class="text-center" style="padding:80px;">
      <div style="font-size:100px;">⚠️</div>
      <h2>حدث خطأ في السيرفر</h2>
      <a href="{{ url_for('dashboard') }}" class="btn btn-primary">← لوحة التحكم</a>
    </div>'''), 500
# ============================================================
#                     لوحة التحكم
# ============================================================

@app.route('/')
@login_required
def dashboard():
    today = date.today()
    month_start, month_end = get_current_month_range()
    future_30 = date.fromordinal(today.toordinal() + 30)

    total_equipment = Equipment.query.count()
    working = Equipment.query.filter_by(status='يعمل').count()
    broken = Equipment.query.filter_by(status='معطل').count()
    in_maintenance = Equipment.query.filter_by(status='صيانة').count()

    total_engineers = Engineer.query.filter_by(active=True).count()
    total_departments = Department.query.count()

    open_maintenance = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.status.in_(['جديد', 'قيد العمل'])
    ).count()
    month_maintenance = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at >= month_start
    ).count()

    month_cost = db.session.query(
        func.coalesce(func.sum(MaintenanceRecord.cost), 0)
    ).filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at >= month_start
    ).scalar()

    open_tickets = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='مفتوح').count()
    month_tickets = Ticket.query.filter(
        Ticket.opened_at >= month_start
    ).count()

    overdue = Equipment.query.filter(
        Equipment.next_maintenance < today,
        Equipment.status != 'مستبعد'
    ).count()

    upcoming = Equipment.query.filter(
        Equipment.next_maintenance <= future_30,
        Equipment.next_maintenance >= today,
        Equipment.status != 'مستبعد'
    ).count()

    low_parts = SparePart.query.filter(
        SparePart.quantity <= SparePart.alert_limit
    ).count()

    recent_maintenance = MaintenanceRecord.query\
        .order_by(MaintenanceRecord.id.desc()).limit(5).all()

    recent_tickets = Ticket.query\
        .order_by(Ticket.id.desc()).limit(5).all()

    top_engineers = db.session.query(
        Engineer.name, func.count(MaintenanceRecord.id)
    ).join(MaintenanceRecord, MaintenanceRecord.assigned_to == Engineer.id)\
        .filter(MaintenanceRecord.status == 'مكتمل')\
        .group_by(Engineer.id)\
        .order_by(func.count(MaintenanceRecord.id).desc()).limit(5).all()

    monthly_labels = []
    monthly_data = []
    for i in range(5, -1, -1):
        m = today.replace(day=1)
        for _ in range(i):
            m = (m - timedelta(days=1)).replace(day=1)
        if m.month == 12:
            next_m = m.replace(year=m.year + 1, month=1, day=1)
        else:
            next_m = m.replace(month=m.month + 1, day=1)
        count = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
            MaintenanceRecord.created_at >= m,
            MaintenanceRecord.created_at < next_m
        ).count()
        monthly_labels.append(m.strftime('%Y-%m'))
        monthly_data.append(count)

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">🏠 لوحة التحكم</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">
          نظرة شاملة على حالة قسم الهندسة الطبية
        </p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ جهاز جديد</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_form') }}" class="btn btn-success">+ أمر صيانة</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card">
          <span class="stat-icon">🏥</span>
          <p>إجمالي الأجهزة</p>
          <h3>{{ total_equipment }}</h3>
          <small style="color:#78909C;">{{ working }} تعمل • {{ broken }} معطلة</small>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card green">
          <span class="stat-icon">✅</span>
          <p>نسبة التوفر</p>
          <h3>{{ "%.0f"|format((working / total_equipment * 100) if total_equipment > 0 else 0) }}%</h3>
          <small style="color:#2E7D32;">من {{ total_equipment }} جهاز</small>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card red">
          <span class="stat-icon">⚠️</span>
          <p>أجهزة معطلة</p>
          <h3>{{ broken }}</h3>
          <small style="color:#C62828;">تحتاج إصلاح فوري</small>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card orange">
          <span class="stat-icon">🔧</span>
          <p>تحت الصيانة</p>
          <h3>{{ in_maintenance }}</h3>
          <small style="color:#F9A825;">قيد العمل</small>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card cyan">
          <span class="stat-icon">📋</span>
          <p>أوامر صيانة مفتوحة</p>
          <h3>{{ open_maintenance }}</h3>
          <small style="color:#00838F;">{{ month_maintenance }} هذا الشهر</small>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card purple">
          <span class="stat-icon">💰</span>
          <p>تكاليف الشهر</p>
          <h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(month_cost) }}</h3>
          <small style="color:#6A1B9A;">{{ get_setting('currency', 'ر.ي') }}</small>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card">
          <span class="stat-icon">👷</span>
          <p>المهندسين</p>
          <h3>{{ total_engineers }}</h3>
          <small style="color:#78909C;">{{ total_departments }} قسم</small>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card red">
          <span class="stat-icon">🎫</span>
          <p>تذاكر مفتوحة</p>
          <h3>{{ open_tickets }}</h3>
          <small style="color:#C62828;">{{ month_tickets }} هذا الشهر</small>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-8">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">
            <span>📈 أوامر الصيانة - آخر 6 أشهر</span>
          </div>
          <div class="card-body">
            <canvas id="chartMain" height="80"></canvas>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-4">
        <div class="card h-100">
          <div class="card-header">🔔 التنبيهات المهمة</div>
          <div class="card-body">
            {% if overdue > 0 %}
            <div style="padding:12px; background:#FFEBEE; border-radius:10px; margin-bottom:10px; border-right:4px solid #C62828;">
              <div style="font-weight:bold; color:#C62828;">🚨 {{ overdue }} صيانة متأخرة</div>
              <small style="color:#C62828;">تحتاج تدخل فوري</small>
            </div>
            {% endif %}
            {% if upcoming > 0 %}
            <div style="padding:12px; background:#FFF8E1; border-radius:10px; margin-bottom:10px; border-right:4px solid #F9A825;">
              <div style="font-weight:bold; color:#F57F17;">⏰ {{ upcoming }} صيانة خلال 30 يوم</div>
            </div>
            {% endif %}
            {% if low_parts > 0 %}
            <div style="padding:12px; background:#FFF8E1; border-radius:10px; margin-bottom:10px; border-right:4px solid #F9A825;">
              <div style="font-weight:bold; color:#F57F17;">📦 {{ low_parts }} قطعة منخفضة</div>
            </div>
            {% endif %}
            {% if broken > 0 %}
            <div style="padding:12px; background:#FFEBEE; border-radius:10px; margin-bottom:10px; border-right:4px solid #C62828;">
              <div style="font-weight:bold; color:#C62828;">⚠️ {{ broken }} جهاز معطل</div>
            </div>
            {% endif %}
            {% if overdue == 0 and upcoming == 0 and low_parts == 0 and broken == 0 %}
            <div style="padding:12px; background:#E8F5E9; border-radius:10px; border-right:4px solid #2E7D32;">
              <div style="font-weight:bold; color:#2E7D32;">✅ كل شيء على ما يرام</div>
            </div>
            {% endif %}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header d-flex justify-content-between">
            <span>🔧 آخر أوامر الصيانة</span>
            <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_list') }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary">عرض الكل</a>
          </div>
          <table class="table mb-0">
            <thead><tr><th>الرقم</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>الحالة</th><th>التاريخ</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>
            {% for m in recent_maintenance %}
              <tr>
                <td><a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_view', id=m.id) }}"><b>{{ m.number }}</b></a></td>
                <td>{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</td>
                <td>
                  {% if m.status == 'مكتمل' %}<span class="badge bg-success">مكتمل</span>
                  {% elif m.status == 'قيد العمل' %}<span class="badge bg-warning">قيد العمل</span>
                  {% else %}<span class="badge bg-secondary">{{ m.status }}</span>{% endif %}
                </td>
                <td>{{ m.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td>
              </tr>
            {% else %}
              <tr><td colspan="4" class="empty-state">
                <div class="icon">🔧</div>
                <h5>لا توجد بيانات</h5>
              </td></tr>
            {% endfor %}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header d-flex justify-content-between">
            <span>🏆 أفضل المهندسين</span>
            <a href="{{ url_for('engineers_list') }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary">عرض الكل</a>
          </div>
          <div class="card-body p-3">
            {% for name, cnt in top_engineers %}
            <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; padding:10px; border-bottom:1px solid #E8ECF1;">
              <div>
                <div style="font-weight:600; color:#1565C0;">👤 {{ name }}</div>
                <div style="font-size:12px; color:#78909C;">{{ cnt }} صيانة مكتملة</div>
              </div>
              <span class="badge bg-success">{{ cnt }}</span>
            </div>
            {% else %}
            <div class="empty-state" style="padding:30px;">
              <div class="icon">🏆</div>
              <h5>لا توجد بيانات</h5>
            </div>
            {% endfor %}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    {% if recent_tickets %}
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-header d-flex justify-content-between">
        <span>🎫 آخر التذاكر</span>
        <a href="{{ url_for('tickets_list') }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary">عرض الكل</a>
      </div>
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr><th>الرقم</th><th>العنوان</th><th>الأولوية</th><th>الحالة</th><th>التاريخ</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for t in recent_tickets %}
          <tr>
            <td><a href="{{ url_for('ticket_view', id=t.id) }}"><b>{{ t.number }}</b></a></td>
            <td>{{ t.title[:40] }}{% if t.title|length > 40 %}...{% endif %}</td>
            <td><span class="priority-{{ t.priority }}">{{ t.priority }}</span></td>
            <td>
              {% if t.status == 'مفتوح' %}<span class="badge bg-danger">مفتوح</span>
              {% elif t.status == 'قيد المعالجة' %}<span class="badge bg-warning">قيد المعالجة</span>
              {% else %}<span class="badge bg-success">{{ t.status }}</span>{% endif %}
            </td>
            <td>{{ t.opened_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td>
          </tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    {% endif %}
    ''',
    total_equipment=total_equipment, working=working, broken=broken,
    in_maintenance=in_maintenance,
    total_engineers=total_engineers, total_departments=total_departments,
    open_maintenance=open_maintenance, month_maintenance=month_maintenance,

        },
        options: {
          responsive: true,
          plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
          scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true } }
        }
      });
    </script>'''


# ============================================================
#                     الأجهزة الطبية
# ============================================================

@app.route('/equipment')
@login_required
def equipment_list():
    q = request.args.get('q', '').strip()
    status = request.args.get('status', '').strip()
    dept = request.args.get('dept', '').strip()
    criticality = request.args.get('criticality', '').strip()
    maintenance_filter = request.args.get('maintenance', '').strip()

    query = Equipment.query

    if q:
        query = query.filter(or_(
            Equipment.name.contains(q),
            Equipment.code.contains(q),
            Equipment.serial_number.contains(q),
            Equipment.manufacturer.contains(q),
        ))
    if status:
        query = query.filter(Equipment.status == status)
    if dept:
        query = query.filter(Equipment.department_id == int(dept))
    if criticality:
        query = query.filter(Equipment.criticality == criticality)

    today = date.today()
    if maintenance_filter == 'overdue':
        query = query.filter(
            Equipment.next_maintenance < today,
            Equipment.status != 'مستبعد'
        )
    elif maintenance_filter == 'upcoming':
        future_30 = date.fromordinal(today.toordinal() + 30)
        query = query.filter(
            Equipment.next_maintenance >= today,
            Equipment.next_maintenance <= future_30
        )

    items = query.order_by(Equipment.id.desc()).all()

    total = Equipment.query.count()
    working = Equipment.query.filter_by(status='يعمل').count()
    broken = Equipment.query.filter_by(status='معطل').count()
    maintenance = Equipment.query.filter_by(status='صيانة').count()

    departments = Department.query.order_by(Department.name).all()

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">🏥 الأجهزة الطبية</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">{{ items|length }} جهاز</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <a href="{{ url_for('export_equipment_csv') }}" class="btn btn-outline-success">📊 CSV</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ جهاز جديد</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan">
        <span class="stat-icon">🏥</span><p>الإجمالي</p><h3>{{ total }}</h3>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card green">
        <span class="stat-icon">✅</span><p>تعمل</p><h3>{{ working }}</h3>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card red">
        <span class="stat-icon">⚠️</span><p>معطلة</p><h3>{{ broken }}</h3>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card orange">
        <span class="stat-icon">🔧</span><p>صيانة</p><h3>{{ maintenance }}</h3>
      </div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-3"><div class="card-body">
      <form class="row g-2">
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <input name="q" value="{{ q }}" class="form-control" placeholder="🔍 بحث...">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-2">
          <select name="status" class="form-select">
            <option value="">كل الحالات</option>
            {% for s in ['يعمل','معطل','صيانة','مخزون','مستبعد'] %}
            <option value="{{ s }}" {{ 'selected' if status==s }}>{{ s }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-2">
          <select name="dept" class="form-select">
            <option value="">كل الأقسام</option>
            {% for d in departments %}
            <option value="{{ d.id }}" {{ 'selected' if dept==d.id|string }}>{{ d.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-2">
          <select name="criticality" class="form-select">
            <option value="">كل الأهميات</option>
            <option value="حرج" {{ 'selected' if criticality=='حرج' }}>حرج</option>
            <option value="مهم" {{ 'selected' if criticality=='مهم' }}>مهم</option>
            <option value="عادي" {{ 'selected' if criticality=='عادي' }}>عادي</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <button class="btn btn-primary w-100">🔍 بحث</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div></div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>الكود</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>الشركة</th><th>القسم</th>
          <th>الأهمية</th><th>الحالة</th><th>الصيانة القادمة</th><th>إجراءات</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for e in items %}
        <tr>
          <td><b>{{ e.code or '-' }}</b></td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_view', id=e.id) }}"><b>{{ e.name }}</b></a>
            {% if e.serial_number %}<br><small class="text-muted">S/N: {{ e.serial_number }}</small>{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>{{ e.manufacturer or '-' }}<br><small class="text-muted">{{ e.model or '' }}</small></td>
          <td>{{ e.department.name if e.department else '-' }}</td>
          <td><span class="priority-{{ e.criticality }}">{{ e.criticality }}</span></td>
          <td><span class="badge-status badge-{{ e.status }}">{{ e.status }}</span></td>
          <td>
            {% if e.next_maintenance %}
              {% if e.next_maintenance < today() %}
                <span style="color:#C62828;"><b>{{ e.next_maintenance }}</b> ⚠️</span>
              {% elif e.next_maintenance <= (today() + timedelta(days=30)) %}
                <span style="color:#F9A825;">{{ e.next_maintenance }}</span>
              {% else %}
                {{ e.next_maintenance }}
              {% endif %}
            {% else %}-{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_view', id=e.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-info" title="عرض">
              <i class="bi bi-eye"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_form', id=e.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" title="تعديل">
              <i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_form') }}?equipment_id={{ e.id }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-warning" title="صيانة">
              <i class="bi bi-wrench"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_print', id=e.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary"
               target="_blank" title="طباعة">
              <i class="bi bi-printer"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_delete', id=e.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
               onclick="return confirm('حذف الجهاز؟')" title="حذف">
              <i class="bi bi-trash"></i></a>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="8" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">🏥</div>
          <h5>لا توجد أجهزة</h5>
          <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ إضافة جهاز</a>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', items=items, q=q, status=status, dept=dept,
        criticality=criticality, maintenance_filter=maintenance_filter,
        total=total, working=working, broken=broken, maintenance=maintenance,
        departments=departments)


@app.route('/equipment/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/equipment/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def equipment_form(id=None):
    e = Equipment.query.get(id) if id else None
    departments = Department.query.order_by(Department.name).all()

    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not e:
            e = Equipment()
            if not request.form.get('code'):
                e.code = next_number(Equipment, 'EQ')
            db.session.add(e)

        new_code = request.form.get('code', '').strip()
        if new_code:
            existing = Equipment.query.filter(
                Equipment.code == new_code,
                Equipment.id != (e.id if e.id else 0)
            ).first()
            if existing:
                flash(f'❌ كود الجهاز "{new_code}" مستخدم مسبقاً', 'danger')
                return redirect(request.url)
            e.code = new_code
        elif not e.code:
            e.code = next_number(Equipment, 'EQ')
        e.name = request.form['name'].strip()
        e.category = request.form.get('category', '').strip()
        e.manufacturer = request.form.get('manufacturer', '').strip()
        e.model = request.form.get('model', '').strip()
        e.serial_number = request.form.get('serial_number', '').strip()
        e.department_id = int(request.form['department_id']) if request.form.get('department_id') else None
        e.location = request.form.get('location', '').strip()
        e.status = request.form.get('status', 'يعمل')
        e.criticality = request.form.get('criticality', 'عادي')
        e.supplier = request.form.get('supplier', '').strip()
        e.price = float(request.form.get('price') or 0)
        e.maintenance_period_days = int(request.form.get('maintenance_period_days') or 180)
        e.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()

        if request.form.get('purchase_date'):
            e.purchase_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['purchase_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        if request.form.get('install_date'):
            e.install_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['install_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        if request.form.get('warranty_end'):
            e.warranty_end = datetime.strptime(request.form['warranty_end'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        if request.form.get('last_maintenance'):
            e.last_maintenance = datetime.strptime(request.form['last_maintenance'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        if request.form.get('next_maintenance'):
            e.next_maintenance = datetime.strptime(request.form['next_maintenance'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()

        file = request.files.get('image')
        if file and file.filename:
            filename = f"eq_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}_{secure_filename(file.filename)}"
            file.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], filename))
            e.image = filename

        manual = request.files.get('manual_file')
        if manual and manual.filename:
            filename = f"manual_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}_{secure_filename(manual.filename)}"
            manual.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], filename))
            e.manual_file = filename

        db.session.commit()
        log_activity('save_equipment', f'حفظ جهاز: {e.name}')
        flash('✅ تم حفظ الجهاز', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('equipment_view', id=e.id))

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between mb-3">
      <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل جهاز' if e else '➕ إضافة جهاز جديد' }}</h3>
      <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary">← رجوع</a>
    </div>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post" enctype="multipart/form-data">
      <h5 style="color:#1565C0;" class="mb-3">📋 معلومات أساسية</h5>
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>اسم الجهاز <span class="text-danger">*</span></label>
          <input name="name" value="{{ e.name if e else '' }}" class="form-control" required
                 placeholder="مثال: جهاز أشعة سينية">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>كود الجهاز</label>
          <input name="code" value="{{ e.code if e else '' }}" class="form-control"
                 placeholder="تلقائي أو يدوي">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>الفئة</label>
          <input name="category" value="{{ e.category if e else '' }}" class="form-control"
                 list="cats" placeholder="أشعة">
          <datalist id="cats">
            <option>أشعة</option><option>مختبر</option><option>عمليات</option>
            <option>عناية مركزة</option><option>قلب</option><option>تنفس</option>
            <option>غسيل كلى</option><option>أسنان</option><option>طوارئ</option>
          </datalist>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>الشركة المصنعة</label>
          <input name="manufacturer" value="{{ e.manufacturer if e else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>الموديل</label>
          <input name="model" value="{{ e.model if e else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>الرقم التسلسلي S/N</label>
          <input name="serial_number" value="{{ e.serial_number if e else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>القسم</label>
          <select name="department_id" class="form-select">
            <option value="">-- اختر --</option>
            {% for d in departments %}
            <option value="{{ d.id }}" {{ 'selected' if e and e.department_id==d.id }}>{{ d.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>الموقع</label>
          <input name="location" value="{{ e.location if e else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>الحالة</label>
          <select name="status" class="form-select">
            {% for s in ['يعمل','معطل','صيانة','مخزون','مستبعد'] %}
            <option {{ 'selected' if e and e.status==s }}>{{ s }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>درجة الأهمية</label>
          <select name="criticality" class="form-select">
            <option value="حرج" {{ 'selected' if e and e.criticality=='حرج' }}>🚨 حرج</option>
            <option value="مهم" {{ 'selected' if e and e.criticality=='مهم' }}>⚠️ مهم</option>
            <option value="عادي" {{ 'selected' if (not e) or e.criticality=='عادي' }}>✓ عادي</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>دورة الصيانة (أيام)</label>
          <input name="maintenance_period_days" type="number" 
                 value="{{ e.maintenance_period_days if e else 180 }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>السعر</label>
          <input name="price" type="number" step="0.01" value="{{ e.price if e else 0 }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
      </div>

      <h5 style="color:#1565C0;" class="mt-4 mb-3">📅 تواريخ</h5>
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <label>تاريخ الشراء</label>
          <input name="purchase_date" type="date" 
                 value="{{ e.purchase_date if e and e.purchase_date else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <label>تاريخ التركيب</label>
          <input name="install_date" type="date"
                 value="{{ e.install_date if e and e.install_date else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <label>نهاية الضمان</label>
          <input name="warranty_end" type="date"
                 value="{{ e.warranty_end if e and e.warranty_end else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <label>آخر صيانة</label>

        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">

      <div class="row g-3">

          <label>صورة الجهاز</label>
          <input name="image" type="file" class="form-control" accept="image/*">

          </small>
          {% endif %}

        <div class="col-md-6">

          <small class="text-muted">
            <a href="{{ url_for('med_upload_v2', filename=e.manual_file) }}" target="_blank">عرض الملف</a>
          </small>
          {% endif %}
        </div>
        <div class="col-12">
          <label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" rows="2" class="form-control">{{ e.notes if e else '' }}</textarea>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-4">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', e=e, departments=departments)


@app.route('/equipment/<int:id>')
@login_required
def equipment_view(id):
    e = Equipment.query.get_or_404(id)
    maintenance_records = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter_by(equipment_id=id)\
        .order_by(MaintenanceRecord.id.desc()).all()
    calibrations = Calibration.query.filter_by(equipment_id=id)\
        .order_by(Calibration.id.desc()).all()
    tickets = Ticket.query.filter_by(equipment_id=id)\
        .order_by(Ticket.id.desc()).limit(10).all()

    total_maintenance = len(maintenance_records)
    total_cost = sum(m.cost for m in maintenance_records)
    completed = sum(1 for m in maintenance_records if m.status == 'مكتمل')

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">
          <i class="bi bi-cpu"></i> {{ e.name }}
          <span class="badge-status badge-{{ e.status }}">{{ e.status }}</span>
        </h3>
        <small class="text-muted">{{ e.code or '' }}</small>
      </div>
      <div>
        <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_form') }}?equipment_id={{ e.id }}" class="btn btn-warning">+ صيانة</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_print', id=e.id) }}" class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_form', id=e.id) }}" class="btn btn-primary">✏️ تعديل</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('equipment_list') }}" class="btn btn-outline-secondary">← رجوع</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card">
        <p>القسم</p><h5>{{ e.department.name if e.department else '-' }}</h5>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan">
        <p>الشركة والموديل</p><h5>{{ e.manufacturer or '-' }}</h5>
        <small>{{ e.model or '' }}</small>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card orange">
        <p>إجمالي الصيانة</p><h3>{{ total_maintenance }}</h3>
        <small>{{ completed }} مكتمل</small>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card purple">
        <p>إجمالي التكاليف</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_cost) }}</h3>
      </div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">📋 التفاصيل</div>
          <div class="card-body">
            <table class="table table-sm mb-0">
              <tr><td><b>الكود:</b></td><td>{{ e.code or '-' }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>الفئة:</b></td><td>{{ e.category or '-' }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>S/N:</b></td><td>{{ e.serial_number or '-' }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>الأهمية:</b></td><td><span class="priority-{{ e.criticality }}">{{ e.criticality }}</span></td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>المورد:</b></td><td>{{ e.supplier or '-' }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>السعر:</b></td><td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(e.price) }}</td></tr>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">📅 التواريخ</div>
          <div class="card-body">
            <table class="table table-sm mb-0">
              <tr><td><b>الشراء:</b></td><td>{{ e.purchase_date or '-' }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>التركيب:</b></td><td>{{ e.install_date or '-' }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>الضمان:</b></td><td>{{ e.warranty_end or '-' }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>آخر صيانة:</b></td><td>{{ e.last_maintenance or '-' }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>الصيانة القادمة:</b></td><td>{{ e.next_maintenance or '-' }}</td></tr>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    {% if e.image or e.manual_file %}
    <div class="card mb-4">
      <div class="card-header">📎 المرفقات</div>
      <div class="card-body">
        {% if e.image %}
        <div class="mb-3">
          <b>صورة الجهاز:</b><br>
          <img src="{{ url_for('med_upload_v2', filename=e.image) }}" 
               style="max-width:100%; max-height:400px; border-radius:8px; margin-top:8px;">
        </div>
        {% endif %}
        {% if e.manual_file %}
        <a href="{{ url_for('med_upload_v2', filename=e.manual_file) }}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-outline-primary">
          <i class="bi bi-file-pdf"></i> تحميل الكتالوج
        </a>
        {% endif %}
      </div>
    </div>
    {% endif %}

    <div class="card mb-4">
      <div class="card-header d-flex justify-content-between">
        <span>🔧 سجل الصيانة ({{ maintenance_records|length }})</span>
        <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_form') }}?equipment_id={{ e.id }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-warning">+ صيانة</a>
      </div>
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>الرقم</th><th>النوع</th><th>الوصف</th><th>الحالة</th>
          <th>التاريخ</th><th>المهندس</th><th>التكلفة</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for m in maintenance_records %}
        <tr>
          <td><a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_view', id=m.id) }}">{{ m.number }}</a></td>
          <td><span class="badge bg-info">{{ m.type }}</span></td>
          <td>{{ m.description[:50] if m.description else '-' }}</td>
          <td>
            {% if m.status == 'مكتمل' %}<span class="badge bg-success">مكتمل</span>
            {% elif m.status == 'قيد العمل' %}<span class="badge bg-warning">قيد العمل</span>
            {% else %}<span class="badge bg-secondary">{{ m.status }}</span>{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>{{ m.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.engineer.name if m.engineer else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(m.cost) }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="7" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">🔧</div>
          <h5>لا يوجد سجل صيانة</h5>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>

    {% if calibrations %}
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-header">🎯 سجل المعايرة ({{ calibrations|length }})</div>
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>الرقم</th><th>التاريخ</th><th>القادمة</th>
          <th>النتيجة</th><th>الشهادة</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for c in calibrations %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ c.number }}</td>
          <td>{{ c.calibration_date }}</td>
          <td>{{ c.next_calibration }}</td>
          <td>
            {% if c.result == 'ناجح' %}<span class="badge bg-success">ناجح</span>
            {% else %}<span class="badge bg-danger">فاشل</span>{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>{{ c.certificate_number or '-' }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    {% endif %}
    ''', e=e, maintenance_records=maintenance_records,
        calibrations=calibrations, tickets=tickets,
        total_maintenance=total_maintenance, total_cost=total_cost, completed=completed)


@app.route('/equipment/<int:id>/delete')
@login_required
@head_required
def equipment_delete(id):
    e = Equipment.query.get_or_404(id)
    if e.image:
        try: os.remove(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], e.image))
        except: pass
    if e.manual_file:
        try: os.remove(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], e.manual_file))
        except: pass
    name = e.name
    db.session.delete(e)
    db.session.commit()
    log_activity('delete_equipment', f'حذف جهاز: {name}')
    flash(f'🗑️ تم حذف الجهاز: {name}', 'warning')
    return redirect(url_for('equipment_list'))


@app.route('/equipment/<int:id>/print')
@login_required
def equipment_print(id):
    e = Equipment.query.get_or_404(id)
    maintenance_records = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter_by(equipment_id=id)\
        .order_by(MaintenanceRecord.id.desc()).all()
    return render_template_string('''
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="ar" dir="rtl">
    <head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>تقرير الجهاز - {{ e.name }}</title>
      <link rel="stylesheet" href="''' + url_for('med_static_v2', filename='css/bootstrap.rtl.min.css') + '''">
      <style>
        body { font-family: Tahoma; padding: 20px; }
        .header { text-align: center; border-bottom: 3px double #1565C0; padding-bottom: 15px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
        @media print { .no-print { display: none !important; } }
        table { font-size: 12px; }
      </style>
    </head>
    <body>
      <div class="no-print text-center mb-3">
        <button onclick="window.print()" class="btn btn-primary">🖨️ طباعة</button>
        <button onclick="window.close()" class="btn btn-secondary">إغلاق</button>
      </div>
      <div class="header">
        <h2>🏥 {{ get_setting('hospital_name', 'المستشفى') }}</h2>
        <p>قسم الهندسة الطبية</p>
        <h3>تقرير الجهاز الطبي</h3>
      </div>
      <table class="table table-bordered">
        <tr><td><b>الاسم:</b></td><td>{{ e.name }}</td><td><b>الكود:</b></td><td>{{ e.code or '-' }}</td></tr>
        <tr><td><b>الفئة:</b></td><td>{{ e.category or '-' }}</td><td><b>الحالة:</b></td><td>{{ e.status }}</td></tr>
        <tr><td><b>الشركة:</b></td><td>{{ e.manufacturer or '-' }}</td><td><b>الموديل:</b></td><td>{{ e.model or '-' }}</td></tr>
        <tr><td><b>S/N:</b></td><td>{{ e.serial_number or '-' }}</td><td><b>الأهمية:</b></td><td>{{ e.criticality }}</td></tr>
        <tr><td><b>القسم:</b></td><td>{{ e.department.name if e.department else '-' }}</td><td><b>الموقع:</b></td><td>{{ e.location or '-' }}</td></tr>
      </table>
      <h4>سجل الصيانة ({{ maintenance_records|length }})</h4>
      <table class="table table-bordered">
        <thead style="background: #1565C0; color: white;">
          <tr><th>الرقم</th><th>النوع</th><th>الحالة</th><th>التاريخ</th><th>التكلفة</th></tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for m in maintenance_records %}
          <tr>
            <td>{{ m.number }}</td>
            <td>{{ m.type }}</td>
            <td>{{ m.status }}</td>
            <td>{{ m.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td>
            <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(m.cost) }}</td>
          </tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </body>
    </html>''', e=e, maintenance_records=maintenance_records)


@app.route('/med_uploads_v2/<filename>')
@login_required
def med_upload_v2(filename):
    return send_from_directory(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], filename)

@app.route('/departments')
@login_required
def departments_list():
    items = Department.query.order_by(Department.name).all()
    equipment_counts = {}
    for d in items:
        equipment_counts[d.id] = Equipment.query.filter_by(department_id=d.id).count()
    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">🏢 الأقسام</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">{{ items|length }} قسم</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <a href="{{ url_for('export_departments_csv') }}" class="btn btn-outline-success">📊 CSV</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('department_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ قسم جديد</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>#</th><th>القسم</th><th>الطابق</th><th>المبنى</th>
          <th>المسؤول</th><th>الأجهزة</th><th>إجراءات</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for d in items %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ d.id }}</td>
          <td><b>{{ d.name }}</b></td>
          <td>{{ d.floor or '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ d.building or '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ d.manager_name or '-' }}<br>
            <small class="text-muted">{{ d.manager_phone or '' }}</small>
          </td>
          <td><span class="badge bg-info">{{ equipment_counts.get(d.id, 0) }}</span></td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('department_form', id=d.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary">
              <i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('department_delete', id=d.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
               onclick="return confirm('حذف القسم؟')">
              <i class="bi bi-trash"></i></a>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="7" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">🏢</div>
          <h5>لا توجد أقسام</h5>
          <a href="{{ url_for('department_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ إضافة قسم</a>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', items=items, equipment_counts=equipment_counts)


@app.route('/departments/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/departments/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def department_form(id=None):
    d = Department.query.get(id) if id else None
    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not d:
            d = Department()
            db.session.add(d)
        d.name = request.form['name'].strip()
        d.floor = request.form.get('floor', '').strip()
        d.building = request.form.get('building', '').strip()
        d.manager_name = request.form.get('manager_name', '').strip()
        d.manager_phone = request.form.get('manager_phone', '').strip()
        d.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('departments_list'))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل قسم' if d else '➕ قسم جديد' }}</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>اسم القسم *</label>
          <input name="name" value="{{ d.name if d else '' }}" class="form-control" required></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>الطابق</label>
          <input name="floor" value="{{ d.floor if d else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>المبنى</label>
          <input name="building" value="{{ d.building if d else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>المسؤول</label>
          <input name="manager_name" value="{{ d.manager_name if d else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>هاتف المسؤول</label>
          <input name="manager_phone" value="{{ d.manager_phone if d else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ d.notes if d else '' }}</textarea></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('departments_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', d=d)


@app.route('/departments/<int:id>/delete')
@login_required
@head_required
def department_delete(id):
    d = Department.query.get_or_404(id)
    if Equipment.query.filter_by(department_id=id).count() > 0:
        flash('❌ لا يمكن حذف قسم فيه أجهزة', 'danger')
        return redirect(url_for('departments_list'))
    db.session.delete(d)
    db.session.commit()
    flash('🗑️ تم الحذف', 'warning')
    return redirect(url_for('departments_list'))

@app.route('/engineers/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/engineers/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def engineer_form(id=None):
    e = Engineer.query.get(id) if id else None
    users = User.query.order_by(User.username).all()

    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not e:
            e = Engineer()
            db.session.add(e)
        e.name = request.form['name'].strip()
        e.employee_number = request.form.get('employee_number', '').strip()
        e.job_title = request.form.get('job_title', '').strip()
        e.specialty = request.form.get('specialty', '').strip()
        e.license_number = request.form.get('license_number', '').strip()
        e.phone = request.form.get('phone', '').strip()
        e.email = request.form.get('email', '').strip()
        e.salary = float(request.form.get('salary') or 0)
        e.active = request.form.get('active') == 'on'
        e.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        e.user_id = int(request.form['user_id']) if request.form.get('user_id') else None
        if request.form.get('hire_date'):
            e.hire_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['hire_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('engineers_list'))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل مهندس' if e else '➕ مهندس جديد' }}</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>الاسم *</label>
          <input name="name" value="{{ e.name if e else '' }}" class="form-control" required></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>الرقم الوظيفي</label>
          <input name="employee_number" value="{{ e.employee_number if e else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>تاريخ التعيين</label>
          <input name="hire_date" type="date" value="{{ e.hire_date if e and e.hire_date else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الوظيفة</label>
          <select name="job_title" class="form-select">
            {% for j in ['رئيس قسم','مهندس أول','مهندس','فني أول','فني','متدرب'] %}
            <option {{ 'selected' if e and e.job_title==j }}>{{ j }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>التخصص</label>
          <input name="specialty" value="{{ e.specialty if e else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الترخيص</label>
          <input name="license_number" value="{{ e.license_number if e else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الهاتف</label>
          <input name="phone" value="{{ e.phone if e else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>البريد</label>
          <input name="email" type="email" value="{{ e.email if e else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الراتب</label>
          <input name="salary" type="number" step="0.01" value="{{ e.salary if e else 0 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>ربط بحساب</label>
          <select name="user_id" class="form-select">
            <option value="">-- بدون --</option>
            {% for u in users %}
            <option value="{{ u.id }}" {{ 'selected' if e and e.user_id==u.id }}>{{ u.username }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-6">
          <label class="form-check mt-4">
            <input type="checkbox" name="active" class="form-check-input" {{ 'checked' if not e or e.active }}>
            نشط
          </label>
        </div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ e.notes if e else '' }}</textarea></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('engineers_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', e=e, users=users)


@app.route('/engineers/<int:id>')
@login_required
def engineer_profile(id):
    e = Engineer.query.get_or_404(id)
    records = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter_by(assigned_to=id)\
        .order_by(MaintenanceRecord.id.desc()).all()
    trainings = TrainingRecord.query.filter_by(engineer_id=id)\
        .order_by(TrainingRecord.id.desc()).all()
    reviews = PerformanceReview.query.filter_by(engineer_id=id)\
        .order_by(PerformanceReview.id.desc()).all()

    total = len(records)
    completed = sum(1 for m in records if m.status == 'مكتمل')
    in_progress = sum(1 for m in records if m.status == 'قيد العمل')
    total_hours = sum(m.duration_hours for m in records if m.duration_hours)

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">👷 {{ e.name }}</h3>
        <small class="text-muted">{{ e.job_title or '' }} • {{ e.specialty or '' }}</small>
      </div>
      <div>
        <a href="{{ url_for('engineer_form', id=e.id) }}" class="btn btn-primary">✏️ تعديل</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('engineers_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary">← رجوع</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan"><p>إجمالي المهام</p><h3>{{ total }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card green"><p>مكتملة</p><h3>{{ completed }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card orange"><p>قيد العمل</p><h3>{{ in_progress }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card purple"><p>ساعات</p><h3>{{ "%.0f"|format(total_hours) }}</h3></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-4">
      <div class="card-header">📋 معلومات</div>
      <div class="card-body">
        <table class="table table-sm mb-0">
          <tr><td><b>الرقم الوظيفي:</b></td><td>{{ e.employee_number or '-' }}</td>
              <td><b>الترخيص:</b></td><td>{{ e.license_number or '-' }}</td></tr>
          <tr><td><b>الهاتف:</b></td><td>{{ e.phone or '-' }}</td>
              <td><b>البريد:</b></td><td>{{ e.email or '-' }}</td></tr>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-4">
      <div class="card-header d-flex justify-content-between">
        <span>🎓 سجل التدريب ({{ trainings|length }})</span>
        <a href="{{ url_for('training_form') }}?engineer_id={{ e.id }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-success">+ تدريب</a>
      </div>
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr><th>الدورة</th><th>المقدم</th><th>التاريخ</th><th>الساعات</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for t in trainings %}
        <tr><td>{{ t.course_name }}</td><td>{{ t.provider or '-' }}</td>
            <td>{{ t.start_date or '-' }}</td><td>{{ t.hours }}</td></tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="4" class="text-center text-muted py-3">لا يوجد تدريب</td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-header">🔧 سجل المهام ({{ total }})</div>
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr><th>الرقم</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>النوع</th><th>الحالة</th><th>التاريخ</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for m in records %}
        <tr>
          <td><a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_view', id=m.id) }}">{{ m.number }}</a></td>
          <td>{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.type }}</td>
          <td>
            {% if m.status == 'مكتمل' %}<span class="badge bg-success">مكتمل</span>
            {% elif m.status == 'قيد العمل' %}<span class="badge bg-warning">قيد العمل</span>
            {% else %}<span class="badge bg-secondary">{{ m.status }}</span>{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>{{ m.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="5" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">🔧</div>
          <h5>لا توجد مهام</h5>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', e=e, records=records, trainings=trainings, reviews=reviews,
        total=total, completed=completed, in_progress=in_progress, total_hours=total_hours)

    e = Engineer.query.get_or_404(id)

@app.route('/training/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/training/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def training_form(id=None):
    t = TrainingRecord.query.get(id) if id else None
    engineers = Engineer.query.filter_by(active=True).all()
    preselected = request.args.get('engineer_id')

    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not t:
            t = TrainingRecord()
            db.session.add(t)
        t.engineer_id = int(request.form['engineer_id'])
        t.course_name = request.form['course_name'].strip()
        t.provider = request.form.get('provider', '').strip()
        t.hours = float(request.form.get('hours') or 0)
        t.certificate_number = request.form.get('certificate_number', '').strip()
        t.cost = float(request.form.get('cost') or 0)
        t.status = request.form.get('status', 'مكتمل')
        t.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        if request.form.get('start_date'):
            t.start_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['start_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        if request.form.get('end_date'):
            t.end_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['end_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('training_list'))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل' if t else '➕ تدريب جديد' }}</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>المهندس *</label>
          <select name="engineer_id" class="form-select" required>
            <option value="">-- اختر --</option>
            {% for e in engineers %}
            <option value="{{ e.id }}" {{ 'selected' if (t and t.engineer_id==e.id) or (not t and preselected==e.id|string) }}>{{ e.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>اسم الدورة *</label>
          <input name="course_name" value="{{ t.course_name if t else '' }}" class="form-control" required></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>مقدم الدورة</label>
          <input name="provider" value="{{ t.provider if t else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>من</label>
          <input name="start_date" type="date" value="{{ t.start_date if t and t.start_date else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>إلى</label>
          <input name="end_date" type="date" value="{{ t.end_date if t and t.end_date else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>الساعات</label>
          <input name="hours" type="number" step="0.5" value="{{ t.hours if t else 0 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>التكلفة</label>
          <input name="cost" type="number" step="0.01" value="{{ t.cost if t else 0 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>رقم الشهادة</label>
          <input name="certificate_number" value="{{ t.certificate_number if t else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>الحالة</label>
          <select name="status" class="form-select">
            {% for s in ['مكتمل', 'قيد التنفيذ', 'مؤجل'] %}
            <option {{ 'selected' if t and t.status==s }}>{{ s }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ t.notes if t else '' }}</textarea></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('training_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', t=t, engineers=engineers, preselected=preselected)
