@app.route('/performance')
@login_required
def performance_list():
    items = PerformanceReview.query.order_by(PerformanceReview.id.desc()).all()
    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">🏆 تقييم الأداء</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">{{ items|length }} تقييم</p>
      </div>
      <a href="{{ url_for('performance_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ تقييم جديد</a>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>#</th><th>المهندس</th><th>التاريخ</th><th>الفترة</th>
          <th>الإجمالي</th><th>التقدير</th><th>إجراءات</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for r in items %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ r.id }}</td>
          <td><b>{{ r.engineer.name if r.engineer else '-' }}</b></td>
          <td>{{ r.review_date }}</td>
          <td>{{ r.period }}</td>
          <td><b>{{ r.total_score }}</b>/20</td>
          <td>
            <span class="badge bg-{{ 'success' if r.rating=='ممتاز' else ('info' if r.rating=='جيد جداً' else ('warning' if r.rating=='جيد' else 'secondary')) }}">
              {{ r.rating }}
            </span>
          </td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('performance_form', id=r.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary"><i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('performance_delete', id=r.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
               onclick="return confirm('حذف؟')"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></a>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="7" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">🏆</div>
          <h5>لا توجد تقييمات</h5>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', items=items)


@app.route('/performance/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/performance/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def performance_form(id=None):
    r = PerformanceReview.query.get(id) if id else None
    engineers = Engineer.query.filter_by(active=True).all()
    preselected = request.args.get('engineer_id')

    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not r:
            r = PerformanceReview()
            db.session.add(r)
        r.engineer_id = int(request.form['engineer_id'])
        r.period = request.form.get('period', '').strip()
        r.quality_score = float(request.form.get('quality_score') or 0)
        r.speed_score = float(request.form.get('speed_score') or 0)
        r.teamwork_score = float(request.form.get('teamwork_score') or 0)
        r.knowledge_score = float(request.form.get('knowledge_score') or 0)
        r.total_score = r.quality_score + r.speed_score + r.teamwork_score + r.knowledge_score

        if r.total_score >= 18:
            r.rating = 'ممتاز'
        elif r.total_score >= 15:
            r.rating = 'جيد جداً'
        elif r.total_score >= 12:
            r.rating = 'جيد'
        elif r.total_score >= 8:
            r.rating = 'مقبول'
        else:
            r.rating = 'ضعيف'

        r.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        if request.form.get('review_date'):
            r.review_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['review_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        else:
            r.review_date = date.today()

        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('performance_list'))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل تقييم' if r else '➕ تقييم جديد' }}</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>المهندس *</label>
          <select name="engineer_id" class="form-select" required>
            <option value="">-- اختر --</option>
            {% for e in engineers %}
            <option value="{{ e.id }}" {{ 'selected' if (r and r.engineer_id==e.id) or (not r and preselected==e.id|string) }}>{{ e.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>التاريخ</label>
          <input name="review_date" type="date" value="{{ r.review_date if r and r.review_date else today() }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>الفترة</label>
          <input name="period" value="{{ r.period if r else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>الجودة (من 5)</label>
          <input name="quality_score" type="number" step="0.5" min="0" max="5" value="{{ r.quality_score if r else 5 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>السرعة (من 5)</label>
          <input name="speed_score" type="number" step="0.5" min="0" max="5" value="{{ r.speed_score if r else 5 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>الفريق (من 5)</label>
          <input name="teamwork_score" type="number" step="0.5" min="0" max="5" value="{{ r.teamwork_score if r else 5 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>المعرفة (من 5)</label>
          <input name="knowledge_score" type="number" step="0.5" min="0" max="5" value="{{ r.knowledge_score if r else 5 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ r.notes if r else '' }}</textarea></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('performance_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', r=r, engineers=engineers, preselected=preselected)


@app.route('/performance/<int:id>/delete')
@login_required
@head_required
def performance_delete(id):
    r = PerformanceReview.query.get_or_404(id)
    db.session.delete(r)
    db.session.commit()
    flash('🗑️ تم الحذف', 'warning')
    return redirect(url_for('performance_list'))
# ============================================================
#                     أوامر الصيانة
# ============================================================

@app.route('/maintenance')
@login_required
def maintenance_list():
    q = request.args.get('q', '').strip()
    status = request.args.get('status', '').strip()
    mtype = request.args.get('type', '').strip()
    priority = request.args.get('priority', '').strip()

    query = MaintenanceRecord.query
    if q:
        query = query.filter(or_(
            MaintenanceRecord.number.contains(q),
            MaintenanceRecord.description.contains(q),
        ))
    if status:
        query = query.filter(MaintenanceRecord.status == status)
    if mtype:
        query = query.filter(MaintenanceRecord.type == mtype)
    if priority:
        query = query.filter(MaintenanceRecord.priority == priority)

    items = query.order_by(MaintenanceRecord.id.desc()).all()

    total = MaintenanceRecord.query.count()
    new_count = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter_by(status='جديد').count()
    in_progress = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter_by(status='قيد العمل').count()
    completed = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter_by(status='مكتمل').count()
    total_cost = db.session.query(
        func.coalesce(func.sum(MaintenanceRecord.cost), 0)
    ).scalar()

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">🔧 أوامر الصيانة</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">{{ items|length }} أمر</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <a href="{{ url_for('export_maintenance_csv') }}" class="btn btn-outline-success">📊 CSV</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ أمر صيانة جديد</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan">
        <span class="stat-icon">📋</span><p>الإجمالي</p><h3>{{ total }}</h3>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card orange">
        <span class="stat-icon">🆕</span><p>جديدة</p><h3>{{ new_count }}</h3>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card purple">
        <span class="stat-icon">⏳</span><p>قيد العمل</p><h3>{{ in_progress }}</h3>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card green">
        <span class="stat-icon">✅</span><p>مكتملة</p><h3>{{ completed }}</h3>
      </div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-12"><div class="stat-card red">
        <span class="stat-icon">💰</span>
        <p>إجمالي تكاليف الصيانة</p>
        <h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_cost) }} {{ get_setting('currency', 'ر.ي') }}</h3>
      </div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-3"><div class="card-body">
      <form class="row g-2">
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <input name="q" value="{{ q }}" class="form-control" placeholder="🔍 بحث...">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-2">
          <select name="status" class="form-select">
            <option value="">كل الحالات</option>
            {% for s in ['جديد','قيد العمل','مكتمل','مؤجل','ملغى'] %}
            <option value="{{ s }}" {{ 'selected' if status==s }}>{{ s }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-2">
          <select name="type" class="form-select">
            <option value="">كل الأنواع</option>
            {% for t in ['وقائية','تصحيحية','طارئة','معايرة'] %}
            <option value="{{ t }}" {{ 'selected' if mtype==t }}>{{ t }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-2">
          <select name="priority" class="form-select">
            <option value="">كل الأولويات</option>
            {% for p in ['طارئ','مستعجل','عادي'] %}
            <option value="{{ p }}" {{ 'selected' if priority==p }}>{{ p }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <button class="btn btn-primary w-100">🔍 بحث</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div></div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>الرقم</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>النوع</th><th>الأولوية</th>
          <th>الحالة</th><th>المهندس</th><th>التكلفة</th><th>التاريخ</th><th>إجراءات</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for m in items %}
        <tr>
          <td><b>{{ m.number }}</b></td>
          <td><a href="{{ url_for('equipment_view', id=m.equipment_id) }}">{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</a></td>
          <td><span class="badge bg-secondary">{{ m.type }}</span></td>
          <td><span class="priority-{{ m.priority }}">{{ m.priority }}</span></td>
          <td>
            {% if m.status == 'مكتمل' %}<span class="badge bg-success">مكتمل</span>
            {% elif m.status == 'قيد العمل' %}<span class="badge bg-warning">قيد العمل</span>
            {% elif m.status == 'جديد' %}<span class="badge bg-info">جديد</span>
            {% else %}<span class="badge bg-dark">{{ m.status }}</span>{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>{{ m.engineer.name if m.engineer else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(m.cost) }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_view', id=m.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-info" title="عرض"><i class="bi bi-eye"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_form', id=m.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" title="تعديل"><i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_print', id=m.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary" target="_blank" title="طباعة"><i class="bi bi-printer"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_delete', id=m.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
               onclick="return confirm('حذف أمر الصيانة؟')" title="حذف"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></a>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="9" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">🔧</div>
          <h5>لا توجد أوامر صيانة</h5>
          <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ إنشاء أمر</a>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', items=items, q=q, status=status, mtype=mtype, priority=priority,
        total=total, new_count=new_count,
        in_progress=in_progress, completed=completed, total_cost=total_cost)


@app.route('/maintenance/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/maintenance/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def maintenance_form(id=None):
    m = MaintenanceRecord.query.get(id) if id else None
    equipment = Equipment.query.order_by(Equipment.name).all()
    engineers = Engineer.query.filter_by(active=True).order_by(Engineer.name).all()
    preselected_eq = request.args.get('equipment_id')

    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not m:
            m = MaintenanceRecord(number=next_number(MaintenanceRecord, 'MT'))
            db.session.add(m)

        m.equipment_id = int(request.form['equipment_id'])
        m.type = request.form.get('type', 'تصحيحية')
        m.description = request.form.get('description', '').strip()
        m.reported_by = request.form.get('reported_by', '').strip()
        m.assigned_to = int(request.form['assigned_to']) if request.form.get('assigned_to') else None
        m.status = request.form.get('status', 'جديد')
        m.priority = request.form.get('priority', 'عادي')
        m.duration_hours = float(request.form.get('duration_hours') or 0)
        m.cost = float(request.form.get('cost') or 0)
        m.parts_used = request.form.get('parts_used', '').strip()
        m.result = request.form.get('result', '').strip()
        m.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()

        if request.form.get('start_date'):
            m.start_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['start_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        if request.form.get('end_date'):
            m.end_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['end_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()

        db.session.commit()

        if m.status == 'مكتمل' and m.equipment:
            m.equipment.status = 'يعمل'
            m.equipment.last_maintenance = date.today()
            if m.equipment.maintenance_period_days:
                m.equipment.next_maintenance = date.fromordinal(
                    date.today().toordinal() + m.equipment.maintenance_period_days
                )
            db.session.commit()
        elif m.status == 'قيد العمل' and m.equipment:
            m.equipment.status = 'صيانة'
            db.session.commit()

        log_activity('save_maintenance', f'حفظ أمر صيانة: {m.number}')
        flash('✅ تم حفظ أمر الصيانة', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('maintenance_view', id=m.id))

    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل أمر صيانة' if m else '➕ أمر صيانة جديد' }}</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-6">
          <label>الجهاز <span class="text-danger">*</span></label>
          <select name="equipment_id" class="form-select" required>
            <option value="">-- اختر --</option>
            {% for eq in equipment %}
            <option value="{{ eq.id }}" {{ 'selected' if (m and m.equipment_id==eq.id) or (not m and preselected_eq==eq.id|string) }}>
              {{ eq.name }} ({{ eq.code or '' }})
            </option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <label>النوع</label>
          <select name="type" class="form-select">
            {% for t in ['وقائية','تصحيحية','طارئة','معايرة'] %}
            <option {{ 'selected' if m and m.type==t }}>{{ t }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <label>الأولوية</label>
          <select name="priority" class="form-select">
            {% for p in ['طارئ','مستعجل','عادي'] %}
            <option {{ 'selected' if m and m.priority==p }}>{{ p }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-12">
          <label>وصف المشكلة</label>
          <textarea name="description" rows="3" class="form-control" required>{{ m.description if m else '' }}</textarea>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>مُبلِّغ</label>
          <input name="reported_by" value="{{ m.reported_by if m else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>المهندس</label>
          <select name="assigned_to" class="form-select">
            <option value="">-- غير محدد --</option>
            {% for e in engineers %}
            <option value="{{ e.id }}" {{ 'selected' if m and m.assigned_to==e.id }}>{{ e.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>الحالة</label>
          <select name="status" class="form-select">
            {% for s in ['جديد','قيد العمل','مكتمل','مؤجل','ملغى'] %}
            <option {{ 'selected' if m and m.status==s }}>{{ s }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>تاريخ البداية</label>
          <input name="start_date" type="date" value="{{ m.start_date if m and m.start_date else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>تاريخ الانتهاء</label>
          <input name="end_date" type="date" value="{{ m.end_date if m and m.end_date else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>المدة (ساعات)</label>
          <input name="duration_hours" type="number" step="0.5" value="{{ m.duration_hours if m else 0 }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <label>التكلفة</label>
          <input name="cost" type="number" step="0.01" value="{{ m.cost if m else 0 }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-8">
          <label>قطع الغيار</label>
          <input name="parts_used" value="{{ m.parts_used if m else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-12">
          <label>نتيجة الإصلاح</label>
          <textarea name="result" rows="2" class="form-control">{{ m.result if m else '' }}</textarea>
        </div>
        <div class="col-12">
          <label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ m.notes if m else '' }}</textarea>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-4">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', m=m, equipment=equipment, engineers=engineers,
        preselected_eq=preselected_eq)


@app.route('/maintenance/<int:id>')
@login_required
def maintenance_view(id):
    m = MaintenanceRecord.query.get_or_404(id)
    images = MaintenanceImage.query.filter_by(maintenance_id=id).all()
    part_usage = PartUsage.query.filter_by(maintenance_id=id).all()
    parts = SparePart.query.order_by(SparePart.name).all()

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">🔧 {{ m.number }}</h3>
        <small class="text-muted">{{ m.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M') }} | {{ m.type }}</small>
      </div>
      <div>
        <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_print', id=m.id) }}" class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_form', id=m.id) }}" class="btn btn-primary">✏️ تعديل</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_list') }}" class="btn btn-outline-secondary">← رجوع</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan">
        <p>الجهاز</p><h5>{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</h5>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card">
        <p>الحالة</p><h5>{{ m.status }}</h5>
        <small>{{ m.priority }}</small>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card purple">
        <p>المهندس</p><h5>{{ m.engineer.name if m.engineer else '-' }}</h5>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card orange">
        <p>التكلفة</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(m.cost) }}</h3>
        <small>{{ m.duration_hours }} ساعة</small>
      </div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-4">
      <div class="card-header">📋 التفاصيل</div>
      <div class="card-body">
        <table class="table table-sm mb-0">
          <tr><td width="20%"><b>الوصف:</b></td><td colspan="3">{{ m.description or '-' }}</td></tr>
          <tr><td><b>مُبلِّغ:</b></td><td>{{ m.reported_by or '-' }}</td>
              <td width="20%"><b>الجهاز:</b></td><td>{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</td></tr>
          <tr><td><b>البداية:</b></td><td>{{ m.start_date or '-' }}</td>
              <td><b>الانتهاء:</b></td><td>{{ m.end_date or '-' }}</td></tr>
          <tr><td><b>النتيجة:</b></td><td colspan="3">{{ m.result or '-' }}</td></tr>
          <tr><td><b>ملاحظات:</b></td><td colspan="3">{{ m.notes or '-' }}</td></tr>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-4 no-print">
      <div class="card-header">📦 قطع الغيار المستخدمة ({{ part_usage|length }})</div>
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>القطعة</th><th>الكمية</th><th>السعر</th><th>الإجمالي</th><th></th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for pu in part_usage %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ pu.part.name if pu.part else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ pu.quantity }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(pu.unit_price) }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(pu.total) }}</td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('part_usage_delete', id=pu.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
               onclick="return confirm('حذف؟')">×</a>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="5" class="text-center text-muted py-3">لا توجد قطع مسجلة</td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <div class="card-body">
        <form method="post" action="{{ url_for('part_usage_add', id=m.id) }}" class="row g-2">
          <div class="col-md-5">
            <select name="part_id" class="form-select" required>
              <option value="">-- اختر القطعة --</option>
              {% for p in parts %}
              <option value="{{ p.id }}">{{ p.name }} (متوفر: {{ p.quantity }})</option>
              {% endfor %}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="col-md-2">
            <input name="quantity" type="number" step="0.01" value="1" class="form-control" placeholder="الكمية">
          </div>
          <div class="col-md-3">
            <input name="unit_price" type="number" step="0.01" class="form-control" placeholder="سعر الوحدة">
          </div>
          <div class="col-md-2">
            <button class="btn btn-primary w-100">+ إضافة</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-4 no-print">
      <div class="card-header">📷 الصور ({{ images|length }})</div>
      <div class="card-body">
        <form method="post" action="{{ url_for('maintenance_upload', id=m.id) }}"
              enctype="multipart/form-data" class="row g-2 mb-3">
          <div class="col-md-3">
            <select name="stage" class="form-select">
              <option value="before">📸 قبل</option>
              <option value="during">📸 أثناء</option>
              <option value="after">📸 بعد</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="col-md-6">
            <input type="file" name="image" class="form-control" accept="image/*" required>
          </div>
          <div class="col-md-3">
            <button class="btn btn-primary w-100">📤 رفع</button>
          </div>
        </form>
        <div class="row g-2">
          {% for img in images %}
          <div class="col-md-3">
            <div class="card">
              <img src="{{ url_for('med_upload_v2', filename=img.filename) }}"
                   style="height:150px;object-fit:cover;">
              <div class="card-body p-2 text-center">
                <small class="badge bg-secondary">
                  {{ {'before':'قبل','during':'أثناء','after':'بعد'}[img.stage] }}
                </small>
                <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_image_delete', id=img.id) }}"
                   class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="return confirm('حذف الصورة؟')">×</a>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          {% endfor %}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    ''', m=m, images=images, part_usage=part_usage, parts=parts)


@app.route('/maintenance/<int:id>/part/add', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def part_usage_add(id):
    maintenance_id = id
    part_id = int(request.form['part_id'])
    qty = float(request.form.get('quantity') or 1)
    price = float(request.form.get('unit_price') or 0)

    if price == 0:
        part = SparePart.query.get(part_id)
        if part:
            price = part.purchase_price

    pu = PartUsage(
        maintenance_id=maintenance_id,
        part_id=part_id,
        quantity=qty,
        unit_price=price,
        total=qty * price
    )
    db.session.add(pu)

    part = SparePart.query.get(part_id)
    if part:
        part.quantity -= qty

    db.session.commit()
    flash('✅ تم إضافة القطعة', 'success')
    return redirect(url_for('maintenance_view', id=maintenance_id))


@app.route('/part_usage/<int:id>/delete')
@login_required
def part_usage_delete(id):
    pu = PartUsage.query.get_or_404(id)
    mid = pu.maintenance_id
    part = SparePart.query.get(pu.part_id)
    if part:
        part.quantity += pu.quantity
    db.session.delete(pu)
    db.session.commit()
    return redirect(url_for('maintenance_view', id=mid))


@app.route('/maintenance/<int:id>/upload', methods=['POST'])
@login_required
def maintenance_upload(id):
    m = MaintenanceRecord.query.get_or_404(id)
    file = request.files.get('image')
    if file and file.filename:
        filename = f"mt_{id}_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}_{secure_filename(file.filename)}"
        file.save(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], filename))
        db.session.add(MaintenanceImage(
            maintenance_id=id,
            stage=request.form['stage'],
            filename=filename
        ))
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم رفع الصورة', 'success')
    return redirect(url_for('maintenance_view', id=id))


@app.route('/maintenance_images/<int:id>/delete')
@login_required
def maintenance_image_delete(id):
    img = MaintenanceImage.query.get_or_404(id)
    mid = img.maintenance_id
    try:
        os.remove(os.path.join(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], img.filename))
    except: pass
    db.session.delete(img)
    db.session.commit()
    return redirect(url_for('maintenance_view', id=mid))


@app.route('/maintenance/<int:id>/delete')
@login_required
@head_required
def maintenance_delete(id):
    m = MaintenanceRecord.query.get_or_404(id)
    number = m.number
    db.session.delete(m)
    db.session.commit()
    log_activity('delete_maintenance', f'حذف أمر صيانة: {number}')
    flash('🗑️ تم الحذف', 'warning')
    return redirect(request.referrer or url_for('maintenance_list'))

@app.route('/maintenance/preventive')
@login_required
def preventive_list():
    items = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter_by(type='وقائية')\
        .order_by(MaintenanceRecord.id.desc()).all()

    total = len(items)
    completed = sum(1 for m in items if m.status == 'مكتمل')
    pending = sum(1 for m in items if m.status in ['جديد', 'قيد العمل'])
    total_cost = sum(m.cost for m in items)

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#2E7D32;">🛡️ الصيانة الوقائية</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">{{ total }} أمر وقائي</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <a href="{{ url_for('preventive_print') }}" class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_form') }}" class="btn btn-success">+ أمر وقائي</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan"><p>الإجمالي</p><h3>{{ total }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card green"><p>مكتملة</p><h3>{{ completed }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card orange"><p>قيد العمل</p><h3>{{ pending }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card purple"><p>التكاليف</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_cost) }}</h3></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>الرقم</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>الوصف</th><th>الحالة</th>
          <th>المهندس</th><th>التكلفة</th><th>التاريخ</th><th>إجراءات</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for m in items %}
        <tr>
          <td><b>{{ m.number }}</b></td>
          <td>{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.description[:60] if m.description else '-' }}</td>
          <td>
            {% if m.status == 'مكتمل' %}<span class="badge bg-success">مكتمل</span>
            {% elif m.status == 'قيد العمل' %}<span class="badge bg-warning">قيد العمل</span>
            {% else %}<span class="badge bg-secondary">{{ m.status }}</span>{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>{{ m.engineer.name if m.engineer else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(m.cost) }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_view', id=m.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-info" title="عرض"><i class="bi bi-eye"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_form', id=m.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" title="تعديل"><i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_delete', id=m.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
               onclick="return confirm('حذف الأمر؟')" title="حذف"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></a>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="8" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">🛡️</div>
          <h5>لا توجد أوامر وقائية</h5>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>

        pending=pending, total_cost=total_cost)

@app.route('/maintenance-plans/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/maintenance-plans/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def maintenance_plan_form(id=None):
    p = MaintenancePlan.query.get(id) if id else None
    equipment = Equipment.query.order_by(Equipment.name).all()

    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not p:
            p = MaintenancePlan()
            db.session.add(p)
        p.equipment_id = int(request.form['equipment_id'])
        p.plan_name = request.form.get('plan_name', '').strip()
        p.period_days = int(request.form.get('period_days') or 180)
        p.estimated_hours = float(request.form.get('estimated_hours') or 1)
        p.checklist = request.form.get('checklist', '').strip()
        p.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        p.active = request.form.get('active') == 'on'

        if request.form.get('last_date'):
            p.last_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['last_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        if request.form.get('next_date'):
            p.next_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['next_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        elif p.last_date:
            p.next_date = date.fromordinal(p.last_date.toordinal() + p.period_days)

        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('maintenance_plans'))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل خطة' if p else '➕ خطة جديدة' }}</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>الجهاز *</label>
          <select name="equipment_id" class="form-select" required>
            <option value="">-- اختر --</option>
            {% for eq in equipment %}
            <option value="{{ eq.id }}" {{ 'selected' if p and p.equipment_id==eq.id }}>{{ eq.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>اسم الخطة</label>
          <input name="plan_name" value="{{ p.plan_name if p else '' }}" class="form-control">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الدورة (أيام)</label>
          <input name="period_days" type="number" value="{{ p.period_days if p else 180 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>المدة (ساعات)</label>
          <input name="estimated_hours" type="number" step="0.5" value="{{ p.estimated_hours if p else 1 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>آخر صيانة</label>
          <input name="last_date" type="date" value="{{ p.last_date if p and p.last_date else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الصيانة القادمة</label>
          <input name="next_date" type="date" value="{{ p.next_date if p and p.next_date else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-8">
          <label class="form-check mt-4">
            <input type="checkbox" name="active" class="form-check-input" {{ 'checked' if not p or p.active }}>
            خطة نشطة
          </label>
        </div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>قائمة الخطوات</label>
          <textarea name="checklist" rows="6" class="form-control">{{ p.checklist if p else '' }}</textarea></div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ p.notes if p else '' }}</textarea></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_plans') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', p=p, equipment=equipment)


@app.route('/maintenance-plans/<int:id>/execute')
@login_required
def maintenance_plan_execute(id):
    p = MaintenancePlan.query.get_or_404(id)
    today = date.today()
    m = MaintenanceRecord(
        number=next_number(MaintenanceRecord, 'MT'),
        equipment_id=p.equipment_id,
        type='وقائية',
        description=f'صيانة دورية - {p.plan_name or ""}',
        status='جديد',
        priority='عادي',
        start_date=today
    )
    db.session.add(m)
    p.last_date = today
    p.next_date = date.fromordinal(today.toordinal() + p.period_days)
    if p.equipment:
        p.equipment.last_maintenance = today
        p.equipment.next_maintenance = p.next_date
    db.session.commit()
    flash(f'✅ تم إنشاء أمر صيانة: {m.number}', 'success')
    return redirect(url_for('maintenance_view', id=m.id))


@app.route('/maintenance-plans/<int:id>/delete')
@login_required
@head_required
def maintenance_plan_delete(id):
    p = MaintenancePlan.query.get_or_404(id)
    db.session.delete(p)
    db.session.commit()
    flash('🗑️ تم الحذف', 'warning')
    return redirect(url_for('maintenance_plans'))


# ============================================================
#                     تذاكر الأعطال
# ============================================================

@app.route('/tickets')
@login_required
def tickets_list():
    q = request.args.get('q', '').strip()
    status = request.args.get('status', '').strip()
    priority = request.args.get('priority', '').strip()
    query = Ticket.query
    if q:
        query = query.filter(or_(
            Ticket.number.contains(q),
            Ticket.title.contains(q)
        ))
    if status:
        query = query.filter(Ticket.status == status)
    if priority:
        query = query.filter(Ticket.priority == priority)
    items = query.order_by(Ticket.id.desc()).all()

    open_count = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='مفتوح').count()
    in_progress = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='قيد المعالجة').count()
    closed = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='مغلق').count()

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">🎫 تذاكر الأعطال</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">{{ items|length }} تذكرة</p>
      </div>
      <a href="{{ url_for('ticket_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ تذكرة جديدة</a>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-4"><div class="stat-card red">
        <span class="stat-icon">🎫</span><p>مفتوحة</p><h3>{{ open_count }}</h3>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-4"><div class="stat-card orange">
        <span class="stat-icon">⏳</span><p>قيد المعالجة</p><h3>{{ in_progress }}</h3>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-4"><div class="stat-card green">
        <span class="stat-icon">✅</span><p>مغلقة</p><h3>{{ closed }}</h3>
      </div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-3"><div class="card-body">
      <form class="row g-2">
        <div class="col-md-4">
          <input name="q" value="{{ q }}" class="form-control" placeholder="🔍 بحث...">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <select name="status" class="form-select">
            <option value="">كل الحالات</option>
            {% for s in ['مفتوح', 'قيد المعالجة', 'مغلق'] %}
            <option value="{{ s }}" {{ 'selected' if status==s }}>{{ s }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <select name="priority" class="form-select">
            <option value="">كل الأولويات</option>
            {% for p in ['طارئ', 'مستعجل', 'عادي'] %}
            <option value="{{ p }}" {{ 'selected' if priority==p }}>{{ p }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-2"><button class="btn btn-primary w-100">🔍</button></div>
      </form>
    </div></div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>الرقم</th><th>العنوان</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>الأولوية</th>
          <th>الحالة</th><th>المهندس</th><th>التاريخ</th><th>إجراءات</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for t in items %}
        <tr>
          <td><b>{{ t.number }}</b></td>
          <td>{{ t.title }}</td>
          <td>{{ t.equipment.name if t.equipment else '-' }}</td>
          <td><span class="priority-{{ t.priority }}">{{ t.priority }}</span></td>
          <td>
            {% if t.status == 'مفتوح' %}<span class="badge bg-danger">مفتوح</span>
            {% elif t.status == 'قيد المعالجة' %}<span class="badge bg-warning">قيد المعالجة</span>
            {% else %}<span class="badge bg-success">مغلق</span>{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>{{ t.engineer.name if t.engineer else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ t.opened_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('ticket_view', id=t.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-info" title="عرض"><i class="bi bi-eye"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('ticket_form', id=t.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" title="تعديل"><i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('ticket_delete', id=t.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
               onclick="return confirm('حذف التذكرة؟')" title="حذف"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></a>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="8" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">🎫</div>
          <h5>لا توجد تذاكر</h5>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', items=items, q=q, status=status, priority=priority,
        open_count=open_count, in_progress=in_progress, closed=closed)


@app.route('/tickets/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/tickets/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def ticket_form(id=None):
    t = Ticket.query.get(id) if id else None
    equipment = Equipment.query.order_by(Equipment.name).all()
    departments = Department.query.order_by(Department.name).all()
    engineers = Engineer.query.filter_by(active=True).all()

    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not t:
            t = Ticket(number=next_number(Ticket, 'TK'))
            db.session.add(t)
        t.title = request.form['title'].strip()
        t.equipment_id = int(request.form['equipment_id']) if request.form.get('equipment_id') else None
        t.department_id = int(request.form['department_id']) if request.form.get('department_id') else None
        t.reported_by = request.form.get('reported_by', '').strip()
        t.description = request.form.get('description', '').strip()
        t.priority = request.form.get('priority', 'عادي')
        t.status = request.form.get('status', 'مفتوح')
        t.assigned_to = int(request.form['assigned_to']) if request.form.get('assigned_to') else None
        t.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('ticket_view', id=t.id))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل تذكرة' if t else '➕ تذكرة جديدة' }}</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-12"><label>العنوان *</label>
          <input name="title" value="{{ t.title if t else '' }}" class="form-control" required></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>الجهاز</label>
          <select name="equipment_id" class="form-select">
            <option value="">-- بدون --</option>
            {% for eq in equipment %}
            <option value="{{ eq.id }}" {{ 'selected' if t and t.equipment_id==eq.id }}>{{ eq.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>القسم</label>
          <select name="department_id" class="form-select">
            <option value="">-- بدون --</option>
            {% for d in departments %}
            <option value="{{ d.id }}" {{ 'selected' if t and t.department_id==d.id }}>{{ d.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>مُبلِّغ</label>
          <input name="reported_by" value="{{ t.reported_by if t else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الأولوية</label>
          <select name="priority" class="form-select">
            {% for p in ['طارئ','مستعجل','عادي'] %}
            <option {{ 'selected' if t and t.priority==p }}>{{ p }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الحالة</label>
          <select name="status" class="form-select">
            {% for s in ['مفتوح','قيد المعالجة','مغلق'] %}
            <option {{ 'selected' if t and t.status==s }}>{{ s }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>المسؤول</label>
          <select name="assigned_to" class="form-select">
            <option value="">-- غير محدد --</option>
            {% for e in engineers %}
            <option value="{{ e.id }}" {{ 'selected' if t and t.assigned_to==e.id }}>{{ e.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>الوصف</label>
          <textarea name="description" rows="3" class="form-control">{{ t.description if t else '' }}</textarea></div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ t.notes if t else '' }}</textarea></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('tickets_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', t=t, equipment=equipment, departments=departments, engineers=engineers)

          <tr><td><b>ملاحظات:</b></td><td colspan="3">{{ t.notes or '-' }}</td></tr>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
    ''', t=t)


@app.route('/tickets/<int:id>/delete')
@login_required
@head_required
def ticket_delete(id):
    t = Ticket.query.get_or_404(id)
    db.session.delete(t)
    db.session.commit()
    log_activity('delete_ticket', f'حذف تذكرة: {t.number}')
    flash('🗑️ تم حذف التذكرة', 'warning')
    return redirect(url_for('tickets_list'))

@app.route('/checklists/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/checklists/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def checklist_form(id=None):

    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not c:
            c = ChecklistTemplate()
            db.session.add(c)
        c.name = request.form['name'].strip()
        c.category = request.form.get('category', '').strip()
        c.equipment_type = request.form.get('equipment_type', '').strip()
        c.items = request.form.get('items', '').strip()
        c.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        c.active = request.form.get('active') == 'on'
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('checklists_list'))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل قالب' if c else '➕ قالب جديد' }}</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">

          <input name="name" value="{{ c.name if c else '' }}" class="form-control" required></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>الفئة</label>
          <input name="category" value="{{ c.category if c else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>نوع الجهاز</label>
          <input name="equipment_type" value="{{ c.equipment_type if c else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-12">
          <label>بنود القائمة (كل بند في سطر)</label>
          <textarea name="items" rows="10" class="form-control" required>{{ c.items if c else '' }}</textarea>
        </div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ c.notes if c else '' }}</textarea></div>
        <div class="col-12">
          <label class="form-check">
            <input type="checkbox" name="active" class="form-check-input" {{ 'checked' if not c or c.active }}>
            قالب نشط
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('checklists_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', c=c)


@app.route('/checklists/<int:id>/print')
@login_required
def checklist_print(id):
    c = ChecklistTemplate.query.get_or_404(id)
    items_list = c.items.split('\n') if c.items else []
    return render_template_string('''
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="ar" dir="rtl">
    <head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>قائمة فحص - {{ c.name }}</title>
      <link rel="stylesheet" href="''' + url_for('med_static_v2', filename='css/bootstrap.rtl.min.css') + '''">
      <style>
        body { font-family: Tahoma; padding: 20px; }
        .header { text-align: center; border-bottom: 3px double #1565C0; padding-bottom: 15px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
        .item { padding: 10px 0; border-bottom: 1px dashed #ddd; }
        .checkbox { display: inline-block; width: 20px; height: 20px; border: 2px solid #333; margin-left: 10px; vertical-align: middle; }
        @media print { .no-print { display: none !important; } }
      </style>
    </head>
    <body>
      <div class="no-print text-center mb-3">
        <button onclick="window.print()" class="btn btn-primary">🖨️ طباعة</button>
        <button onclick="window.close()" class="btn btn-secondary">إغلاق</button>
      </div>
      <div class="header">
        <h2>🏥 {{ get_setting('hospital_name', 'المستشفى') }}</h2>
        <p>قسم الهندسة الطبية</p>
        <h3>قائمة فحص: {{ c.name }}</h3>
        {% if c.equipment_type %}<p>نوع الجهاز: {{ c.equipment_type }}</p>{% endif %}
        <p>التاريخ: {{ today() }}</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        {% for item in items_list %}
          {% if item.strip() %}
          <div class="item">
            <span class="checkbox"></span>
            <b>{{ item }}</b>
          </div>
          {% endif %}
        {% endfor %}
      </div>
      <div style="margin-top: 40px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
        <div style="text-align: center;"><p><b>المهندس المسؤول</b></p><p>_________________</p></div>
        <div style="text-align: center;"><p><b>رئيس القسم</b></p><p>_________________</p></div>
      </div>
    </body>
    </html>''', c=c, items_list=items_list)

        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', items=items)


@app.route('/calibrations/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def calibration_form():
    equipment = Equipment.query.order_by(Equipment.name).all()
    preselected = request.args.get('equipment_id')
    if request.method == 'POST':
        c = Calibration(number=next_number(Calibration, 'CAL'))
        c.equipment_id = int(request.form['equipment_id'])
        c.result = request.form.get('result', 'ناجح')
        c.certificate_number = request.form.get('certificate_number', '').strip()
        c.calibrated_by = request.form.get('calibrated_by', '').strip()
        c.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        if request.form.get('calibration_date'):
            c.calibration_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['calibration_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        if request.form.get('next_calibration'):
            c.next_calibration = datetime.strptime(request.form['next_calibration'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        db.session.add(c)
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('calibrations_list'))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">➕ معايرة جديدة</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>الجهاز *</label>
          <select name="equipment_id" class="form-select" required>
            <option value="">-- اختر --</option>
            {% for eq in equipment %}
            <option value="{{ eq.id }}" {{ 'selected' if preselected==eq.id|string }}>{{ eq.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>تاريخ المعايرة</label>
          <input name="calibration_date" type="date" class="form-control" required></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>القادمة</label>
          <input name="next_calibration" type="date" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>النتيجة</label>
          <select name="result" class="form-select">
            <option value="ناجح">✅ ناجح</option>
            <option value="فاشل">❌ فاشل</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>رقم الشهادة</label>
          <input name="certificate_number" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>المعاير</label>
          <input name="calibrated_by" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control"></textarea></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('calibrations_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', equipment=equipment, preselected=preselected)


@app.route('/calibrations/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def calibration_edit(id):
    cal = Calibration.query.get_or_404(id)
    equipment = Equipment.query.order_by(Equipment.name).all()
    if request.method == 'POST':
        cal.equipment_id = int(request.form['equipment_id'])
        cal.result = request.form.get('result', 'ناجح')
        cal.certificate_number = request.form.get('certificate_number', '').strip()
        cal.calibrated_by = request.form.get('calibrated_by', '').strip()
        cal.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        if request.form.get('calibration_date'):
            cal.calibration_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['calibration_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        if request.form.get('next_calibration'):
            cal.next_calibration = datetime.strptime(request.form['next_calibration'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم التحديث', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('calibrations_list'))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">✏️ تعديل معايرة</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>الجهاز *</label>
          <select name="equipment_id" class="form-select" required>
            {% for eq in equipment %}
            <option value="{{ eq.id }}" {{ 'selected' if cal.equipment_id==eq.id }}>{{ eq.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>تاريخ المعايرة</label>
          <input name="calibration_date" type="date" value="{{ cal.calibration_date or '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>القادمة</label>
          <input name="next_calibration" type="date" value="{{ cal.next_calibration or '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>النتيجة</label>
          <select name="result" class="form-select">
            <option value="ناجح" {{ 'selected' if cal.result=='ناجح' }}>✅ ناجح</option>
            <option value="فاشل" {{ 'selected' if cal.result=='فاشل' }}>❌ فاشل</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>رقم الشهادة</label>
          <input name="certificate_number" value="{{ cal.certificate_number or '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>المعاير</label>
          <input name="calibrated_by" value="{{ cal.calibrated_by or '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ cal.notes or '' }}</textarea></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('calibrations_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', cal=cal, equipment=equipment)

# ============================================================
#                     قطع الغيار
# ============================================================

@app.route('/parts')
@login_required
def parts_list():
    q = request.args.get('q', '').strip()
    low_only = request.args.get('low') == '1'
    query = SparePart.query
    if q:
        query = query.filter(or_(
            SparePart.name.contains(q),
            SparePart.code.contains(q),
            SparePart.manufacturer.contains(q)
        ))
    if low_only:
        query = query.filter(SparePart.quantity <= SparePart.alert_limit)
    items = query.order_by(SparePart.id.desc()).all()

    total_value = sum(p.quantity * p.purchase_price for p in items)
    low_count = SparePart.query.filter(SparePart.quantity <= SparePart.alert_limit).count()

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">📦 قطع الغيار</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">{{ items|length }} قطعة</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <a href="{{ url_for('export_parts_csv') }}" class="btn btn-outline-success">📊 CSV</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('part_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ قطعة جديدة</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-4"><div class="stat-card cyan">
        <span class="stat-icon">📦</span><p>إجمالي القطع</p><h3>{{ items|length }}</h3>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-4"><div class="stat-card green">
        <span class="stat-icon">💰</span><p>قيمة المخزون</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_value) }}</h3>
      </div></div>
      <div class="col-md-4"><div class="stat-card {{ 'red' if low_count > 0 else 'green' }}">
        <span class="stat-icon">⚠️</span><p>تحت الحد</p><h3>{{ low_count }}</h3>
      </div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-3"><div class="card-body">
      <form class="row g-2">
        <div class="col-md-6">
          <input name="q" value="{{ q }}" class="form-control" placeholder="🔍 بحث...">
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <label class="form-check mt-2">
            <input type="checkbox" name="low" value="1" {{ 'checked' if low_only }}>
            المنخفضة فقط
          </label>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3">
          <button class="btn btn-primary w-100">🔍 بحث</button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div></div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>#</th><th>الكود</th><th>الاسم</th><th>الفئة</th>
          <th>الكمية</th><th>سعر</th><th>القيمة</th><th>إجراءات</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for p in items %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ p.id }}</td>
          <td><b>{{ p.code or '-' }}</b></td>
          <td>
            {{ p.name }}
            {% if p.compatible_with %}<br><small class="text-muted">{{ p.compatible_with }}</small>{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>{{ p.category or '-' }}</td>
          <td>
            <b>{{ p.quantity }}</b> {{ p.unit }}
            {% if p.quantity <= p.alert_limit %}<i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle text-danger"></i>{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(p.purchase_price) }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(p.quantity * p.purchase_price) }}</td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('part_print', id=p.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary" target="_blank" title="طباعة">
              <i class="bi bi-printer"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('part_form', id=p.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" title="تعديل">
              <i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('part_delete', id=p.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
               onclick="return confirm('حذف القطعة؟')" title="حذف">
              <i class="bi bi-trash"></i></a>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="8" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">📦</div>
          <h5>لا توجد قطع غيار</h5>
          <a href="{{ url_for('part_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ إضافة قطعة</a>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', items=items, q=q, low_only=low_only,
        total_value=total_value, low_count=low_count)


@app.route('/parts/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/parts/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def part_form(id=None):
    p = SparePart.query.get(id) if id else None
    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not p:
            p = SparePart()
            if not request.form.get('code'):
                p.code = next_number(SparePart, 'SP')
            db.session.add(p)
        p.code = request.form.get('code', '').strip() or p.code
        p.name = request.form['name'].strip()
        p.category = request.form.get('category', '').strip()
        p.compatible_with = request.form.get('compatible_with', '').strip()
        p.manufacturer = request.form.get('manufacturer', '').strip()
        p.supplier = request.form.get('supplier', '').strip()
        p.unit = request.form.get('unit', 'قطعة')
        p.purchase_price = float(request.form.get('purchase_price') or 0)
        p.quantity = float(request.form.get('quantity') or 0)
        p.alert_limit = float(request.form.get('alert_limit') or 5)
        p.location = request.form.get('location', '').strip()
        p.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('parts_list'))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل قطعة' if p else '➕ قطعة غيار جديدة' }}</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>الاسم *</label>
          <input name="name" value="{{ p.name if p else '' }}" class="form-control" required></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>الكود</label>
          <input name="code" value="{{ p.code if p else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الفئة</label>
          <input name="category" value="{{ p.category if p else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الماركة</label>
          <input name="manufacturer" value="{{ p.manufacturer if p else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الوحدة</label>
          <input name="unit" value="{{ p.unit if p else 'قطعة' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>متوافق مع أجهزة</label>
          <input name="compatible_with" value="{{ p.compatible_with if p else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>سعر الشراء</label>
          <input name="purchase_price" type="number" step="0.01" value="{{ p.purchase_price if p else 0 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الكمية</label>
          <input name="quantity" type="number" step="0.01" value="{{ p.quantity if p else 0 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>حد التنبيه</label>
          <input name="alert_limit" type="number" step="0.01" value="{{ p.alert_limit if p else 5 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>المورد</label>
          <input name="supplier" value="{{ p.supplier if p else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>الموقع</label>
          <input name="location" value="{{ p.location if p else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ p.notes if p else '' }}</textarea></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('parts_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', p=p)

    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">🚚 الموردون</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">{{ items|length }} مورد</p>
      </div>
      <a href="{{ url_for('supplier_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ مورد جديد</a>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>#</th><th>الاسم</th><th>المسؤول</th><th>الهاتف</th>
          <th>الفئة</th><th>التقييم</th><th>إجراءات</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for s in items %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ s.id }}</td>
          <td><b>{{ s.name }}</b></td>
          <td>{{ s.contact_person or '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ s.phone or '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ s.category or '-' }}</td>
          <td>
            {% for i in range(s.rating or 0) %}⭐{% endfor %}
          </td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('supplier_contacts', id=s.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-info" title="سجل التواصل">
              <i class="bi bi-chat-dots"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('supplier_form', id=s.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" title="تعديل">
              <i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('supplier_delete', id=s.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
               onclick="return confirm('حذف المورد؟')" title="حذف">
              <i class="bi bi-trash"></i></a>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="7" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">🚚</div>
          <h5>لا يوجد موردون</h5>
          <a href="{{ url_for('supplier_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ إضافة مورد</a>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', items=items)


@app.route('/suppliers/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/suppliers/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def supplier_form(id=None):
    s = Supplier.query.get(id) if id else None
    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not s:
            s = Supplier()
            db.session.add(s)
        s.name = request.form['name'].strip()
        s.contact_person = request.form.get('contact_person', '').strip()
        s.phone = request.form.get('phone', '').strip()
        s.phone2 = request.form.get('phone2', '').strip()
        s.email = request.form.get('email', '').strip()
        s.address = request.form.get('address', '').strip()
        s.category = request.form.get('category', '').strip()
        s.rating = int(request.form.get('rating') or 3)
        s.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('suppliers_list'))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل مورد' if s else '➕ مورد جديد' }}</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>اسم المورد *</label>
          <input name="name" value="{{ s.name if s else '' }}" class="form-control" required></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>الشخص المسؤول</label>
          <input name="contact_person" value="{{ s.contact_person if s else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الهاتف</label>
          <input name="phone" value="{{ s.phone if s else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>هاتف إضافي</label>
          <input name="phone2" value="{{ s.phone2 if s else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>البريد</label>
          <input name="email" type="email" value="{{ s.email if s else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-8"><label>العنوان</label>
          <input name="address" value="{{ s.address if s else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الفئة</label>
          <input name="category" value="{{ s.category if s else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>التقييم</label>
          <input name="rating" type="number" min="1" max="5" value="{{ s.rating if s else 3 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ s.notes if s else '' }}</textarea></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('suppliers_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', s=s)

@login_required
@head_required
def supplier_delete(id):
    s = Supplier.query.get_or_404(id)
    db.session.delete(s)
    db.session.commit()
    flash('🗑️ تم الحذف', 'warning')
    return redirect(url_for('suppliers_list'))


@app.route('/suppliers/<int:id>/contacts')

def supplier_contacts(id):

        .order_by(SupplierContact.id.desc()).all()
    return render('''

      <div>

        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">{{ items|length }} سجل</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <a href="{{ url_for('supplier_contact_form') }}?supplier_id={{ s.id }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ سجل جديد</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('suppliers_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary">← رجوع</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>التاريخ</th><th>الموضوع</th><th>النوع</th><th>التفاصيل</th><th>متابعة</th><th></th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for c in items %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ c.contact_date }}</td>
          <td><b>{{ c.subject or '-' }}</b></td>
          <td><span class="badge bg-info">{{ c.contact_type or '-' }}</span></td>
          <td>{{ c.details or '-' }}</td>
          <td>
            {% if c.follow_up %}<span class="badge bg-warning">{{ c.follow_up }}</span>{% else %}-{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('supplier_contact_delete', id=c.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
               onclick="return confirm('حذف؟')"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></a>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="6" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">📞</div>
          <h5>لا توجد سجلات تواصل</h5>
          <p>اضغط + سجل جديد لإضافة أول سجل</p>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', s=s, items=items)


@app.route('/supplier-contacts/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def supplier_contact_form():
    suppliers = Supplier.query.order_by(Supplier.name).all()
    preselected = request.args.get('supplier_id')

    if request.method == 'POST':
        c = SupplierContact()
        c.supplier_id = int(request.form['supplier_id'])
        c.subject = request.form.get('subject', '').strip()
        c.details = request.form.get('details', '').strip()
        c.contact_type = request.form.get('contact_type', '').strip()
        if request.form.get('contact_date'):
            c.contact_date = datetime.strptime(request.form['contact_date'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        if request.form.get('follow_up'):
            c.follow_up = datetime.strptime(request.form['follow_up'], '%Y-%m-%d').date()
        db.session.add(c)
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('supplier_contacts', id=c.supplier_id))

    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">➕ سجل تواصل جديد</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>المورد *</label>
          <select name="supplier_id" class="form-select" required>
            <option value="">-- اختر المورد --</option>
            {% for s in suppliers %}
            <option value="{{ s.id }}" {{ 'selected' if preselected==s.id|string }}>{{ s.name }}</option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
          {% if not suppliers %}
          <small class="text-danger">⚠️ لا يوجد موردون. أضف مورداً أولاً من صفحة الموردين.</small>
          {% endif %}
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>التاريخ</label>
          <input name="contact_date" type="date" value="{{ today() }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-3"><label>نوع التواصل</label>
          <select name="contact_type" class="form-select">
            <option>مكالمة هاتفية</option>
            <option>بريد إلكتروني</option>
            <option>زيارة</option>
            <option>واتساب</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>الموضوع</label>
          <input name="subject" class="form-control" placeholder="مثال: طلب عرض سعر"></div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>التفاصيل</label>
          <textarea name="details" rows="4" class="form-control"></textarea></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>تاريخ المتابعة</label>
          <input name="follow_up" type="date" class="form-control"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('suppliers_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', suppliers=suppliers, preselected=preselected)
