# ============================================================
#                     الميزانية
# ============================================================

@app.route('/budget')
@login_required
def budget_list():
    year = request.args.get('year', date.today().year, type=int)
    items = Budget.query.filter_by(year=year).order_by(Budget.category).all()

    total_allocated = sum(b.allocated for b in items)
    total_spent = sum(b.spent for b in items)
    remaining = total_allocated - total_spent
    spent_percent = (total_spent / total_allocated * 100) if total_allocated > 0 else 0

    years = sorted(set([b.year for b in Budget.query.all()]), reverse=True)
    if year not in years:
        years.insert(0, year)

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">💰 الميزانية</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">سنة {{ year }}</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <form method="get" class="d-inline">
          <select name="year" class="form-select d-inline-block" style="width:auto;" onchange="this.form.submit()">
            {% for y in years %}<option value="{{ y }}" {{ 'selected' if y==year }}>{{ y }}</option>{% endfor %}
          </select>
        </form>
        <a href="{{ url_for('budget_print') }}?year={{ year }}" class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
        <a href="{{ url_for('budget_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ بند</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan"><p>المخصص</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_allocated) }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card red"><p>المصروف</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_spent) }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card green"><p>المتبقي</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(remaining) }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card orange"><p>النسبة</p><h3>{{ "%.1f"|format(spent_percent) }}%</h3></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>#</th><th>الفئة</th><th>الوصف</th><th>المخصص</th>
          <th>المصروف</th><th>المتبقي</th><th>النسبة</th><th>إجراءات</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for b in items %}
        {% set remaining_row = b.allocated - b.spent %}
        {% set pct = (b.spent / b.allocated * 100) if b.allocated > 0 else 0 %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ b.id }}</td>
          <td><b>{{ b.category or '-' }}</b></td>
          <td>{{ b.description or '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(b.allocated) }}</td>
          <td class="text-danger">{{ "{:,.0f}".format(b.spent) }}</td>
          <td class="{{ 'text-danger' if remaining_row < 0 else 'text-success' }}">
            {{ "{:,.0f}".format(remaining_row) }}
          </td>
          <td>
            <div style="background:#E8ECF1; height:8px; border-radius:4px; width:100px;">
              <div style="background: {{ '#C62828' if pct > 90 else ('#F9A825' if pct > 70 else '#2E7D32') }}; 
                          width:{{ pct if pct <= 100 else 100 }}%; height:100%; border-radius:4px;"></div>
            </div>
            <small>{{ "%.0f"|format(pct) }}%</small>
          </td>
          <td>
            <a href="{{ url_for('budget_form', id=b.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary"><i class="bi bi-pencil"></i></a>
            <a href="{{ url_for('budget_delete', id=b.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-danger"
               onclick="return confirm('حذف البند؟')"><i class="bi bi-trash"></i></a>
          </td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="8" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">💰</div>
          <h5>لا توجد بنود ميزانية لسنة {{ year }}</h5>
          <a href="{{ url_for('budget_form') }}" class="btn btn-primary">+ إضافة بند</a>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', items=items, year=year, years=years,
        total_allocated=total_allocated, total_spent=total_spent,
        remaining=remaining, spent_percent=spent_percent)


@app.route('/budget/new', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@app.route('/budget/<int:id>/edit', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def budget_form(id=None):
    b = Budget.query.get(id) if id else None
    if request.method == 'POST':
        if not b:
            b = Budget()
            db.session.add(b)
        b.year = int(request.form['year'])
        b.category = request.form.get('category', '').strip()
        b.description = request.form.get('description', '').strip()
        b.allocated = float(request.form.get('allocated') or 0)
        b.spent = float(request.form.get('spent') or 0)
        b.notes = request.form.get('notes', '').strip()
        db.session.commit()
        flash('✅ تم الحفظ', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('budget_list'))
    return render('''
    <h3 style="color:#1565C0;">{{ '✏️ تعديل بند' if b else '➕ بند ميزانية جديد' }}</h3>
    <div class="card p-4"><form method="post">
      <div class="row g-3">
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>السنة *</label>
          <input name="year" type="number" value="{{ b.year if b else today().year }}" class="form-control" required></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الفئة</label>
          <input name="category" value="{{ b.category if b else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>الوصف</label>
          <input name="description" value="{{ b.description if b else '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>المخصص</label>
          <input name="allocated" type="number" step="0.01" value="{{ b.allocated if b else 0 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-6"><label>المصروف</label>
          <input name="spent" type="number" step="0.01" value="{{ b.spent if b else 0 }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-12"><label>ملاحظات</label>
          <textarea name="notes" class="form-control">{{ b.notes if b else '' }}</textarea></div>
      </div>
      <div class="mt-3">
        <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ</button>
        <a href="{{ url_for('budget_list') }}" class="btn btn-secondary btn-lg">إلغاء</a>
      </div>
    </form></div>''', b=b)


@app.route('/budget/print')
@login_required
def budget_print():
    year = request.args.get('year', date.today().year, type=int)
    items = Budget.query.filter_by(year=year).order_by(Budget.category).all()
    total_allocated = sum(b.allocated for b in items)
    total_spent = sum(b.spent for b in items)
    return render_template_string('''
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="ar" dir="rtl">
    <head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>الميزانية {{ year }}</title>
      <link rel="stylesheet" href="''' + url_for('med_static_v2', filename='css/bootstrap.rtl.min.css') + '''">
      <style>body { font-family: Tahoma; padding: 20px; }
        .header { text-align: center; border-bottom: 3px double #1565C0; padding-bottom: 15px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
        @media print { .no-print { display: none !important; } }</style>
    </head>
    <body>
      <div class="no-print text-center mb-3">
        <button onclick="window.print()" class="btn btn-primary">🖨️ طباعة</button>
        <button onclick="window.close()" class="btn btn-secondary">إغلاق</button>
      </div>
      <div class="header">
        <h2>🏥 {{ get_setting('hospital_name', 'المستشفى') }}</h2>
        <p>قسم الهندسة الطبية</p>
        <h3>الميزانية السنوية - {{ year }}</h3>
      </div>
      <table class="table table-bordered">
        <thead style="background:#1565C0; color:white;">
          <tr><th>#</th><th>الفئة</th><th>الوصف</th><th>المخصص</th><th>المصروف</th><th>المتبقي</th></tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for b in items %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ loop.index }}</td>
          <td>{{ b.category }}</td>
          <td>{{ b.description or '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(b.allocated) }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(b.spent) }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(b.allocated - b.spent) }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
        <tfoot style="background:#F5F7FA; font-weight:bold;">
          <tr><td colspan="3">الإجمالي</td>
              <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_allocated) }}</td>
              <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_spent) }}</td>
              <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_allocated - total_spent) }}</td></tr>
        </tfoot>
      </table>
    </body>
    </html>''', items=items, year=year,
        total_allocated=total_allocated, total_spent=total_spent)

# ============================================================
#                     التقارير الشاملة
# ============================================================

@app.route('/reports')
@login_required
def reports():
    from_date = request.args.get('from', (date.today().replace(day=1)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    to_date = request.args.get('to', date.today().strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    try:
        fd = datetime.strptime(from_date, '%Y-%m-%d')
        td = datetime.strptime(to_date, '%Y-%m-%d').replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59)
    except ValueError:
        fd = datetime.combine(date.today().replace(day=1), datetime.min.time())
        td = datetime.now()

    maintenances = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at.between(fd, td)
    ).all()
    total_maintenance = len(maintenances)
    completed = sum(1 for m in maintenances if m.status == 'مكتمل')
    in_progress = sum(1 for m in maintenances if m.status == 'قيد العمل')
    total_cost = sum(m.cost for m in maintenances)

    by_type = {}
    for m in maintenances:
        by_type[m.type] = by_type.get(m.type, 0) + 1

    by_department = {}
    for m in maintenances:
        if m.equipment and m.equipment.department:
            name = m.equipment.department.name
            by_department[name] = by_department.get(name, 0) + 1

    by_engineer = {}
    for m in maintenances:
        if m.engineer:
            name = m.engineer.name
            by_engineer[name] = by_engineer.get(name, 0) + 1

    by_equipment = {}
    for m in maintenances:
        if m.equipment:
            name = m.equipment.name
            by_equipment[name] = by_equipment.get(name, 0) + 1

    top_engineers = sorted(by_engineer.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:10]
    top_equipment = sorted(by_equipment.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:10]

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">📊 التقارير الشاملة</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">من {{ from_date }} إلى {{ to_date }}</p>
      </div>
      <a href="{{ url_for('reports_print') }}?from={{ from_date }}&to={{ to_date }}" 
         class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-3"><div class="card-body">
      <form class="row g-2">
        <div class="col-md-4"><label>من</label>
          <input type="date" name="from" value="{{ from_date }}" class="form-control"></div>

        </div>
      </form>
    </div></div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">

    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">

              </div>
              <div style="background:#E8ECF1; height:8px; border-radius:4px; margin-top:4px;">

              </div>
            </div>
            {% endfor %}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="col-md-6">

      </div>

      <div class="col-md-6">

            {% endfor %}

        <a href="{{ url_for('download_backup') }}" class="btn btn-primary">💾 نسخة احتياطية</a>
      </div>
    </div>
    ''', from_date=from_date, to_date=to_date,
        total_maintenance=total_maintenance, completed=completed,
        in_progress=in_progress, total_cost=total_cost,
        by_type=by_type, by_department=by_department,
        top_engineers=top_engineers, top_equipment=top_equipment)


@app.route('/reports/print')
@login_required
def reports_print():
    from_date = request.args.get('from', (date.today().replace(day=1)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    to_date = request.args.get('to', date.today().strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    try:
        fd = datetime.strptime(from_date, '%Y-%m-%d')
        td = datetime.strptime(to_date, '%Y-%m-%d').replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59)
    except ValueError:
        fd = datetime.combine(date.today().replace(day=1), datetime.min.time())
        td = datetime.now()

    maintenances = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at.between(fd, td)
    ).all()
    total = len(maintenances)
    completed = sum(1 for m in maintenances if m.status == 'مكتمل')
    total_cost = sum(m.cost for m in maintenances)

    return render_template_string('''
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="ar" dir="rtl">
    <head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>التقارير الشاملة</title>
      <link rel="stylesheet" href="''' + url_for('med_static_v2', filename='css/bootstrap.rtl.min.css') + '''">
      <style>body { font-family: Tahoma; padding: 20px; }
        .header { text-align: center; border-bottom: 3px double #1565C0; padding-bottom: 15px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
        @media print { .no-print { display: none !important; } }</style>
    </head>
    <body>
      <div class="no-print text-center mb-3">
        <button onclick="window.print()" class="btn btn-primary">🖨️ طباعة</button>
        <button onclick="window.close()" class="btn btn-secondary">إغلاق</button>
      </div>
      <div class="header">
        <h2>🏥 {{ get_setting('hospital_name', 'المستشفى') }}</h2>
        <p>قسم الهندسة الطبية</p>
        <h3>التقرير الشامل</h3>
        <p>من {{ from_date }} إلى {{ to_date }}</p>
      </div>
      <table class="table table-bordered">
        <tr>
          <td><b>إجمالي المهام:</b></td><td>{{ total }}</td>
          <td><b>مكتملة:</b></td><td>{{ completed }}</td>
          <td><b>التكاليف:</b></td><td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_cost) }}</td>
        </tr>
      </table>
      <h4>تفاصيل المهام</h4>
      <table class="table table-bordered">
        <thead style="background:#1565C0; color:white;">
          <tr><th>#</th><th>الرقم</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>النوع</th><th>الحالة</th><th>المهندس</th><th>التكلفة</th><th>التاريخ</th></tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for m in maintenances %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ loop.index }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.number }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.type }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.status }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.engineer.name if m.engineer else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(m.cost) }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </body>
    </html>''', maintenances=maintenances,
        from_date=from_date, to_date=to_date,
        total=total, completed=completed, total_cost=total_cost)


# ============================================================
#                     التقرير الشهري
# ============================================================

@app.route('/reports/monthly')
@login_required
def report_monthly():
    today = date.today()
    year = request.args.get('year', today.year, type=int)
    month = request.args.get('month', today.month, type=int)

    start = date(year, month, 1)
    if month == 12:
        end = date(year + 1, 1, 1) - timedelta(days=1)
    else:
        end = date(year, month + 1, 1) - timedelta(days=1)

    maintenances = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at >= start,
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at <= datetime.combine(end, datetime.max.time())
    ).all()

    total = len(maintenances)
    completed = sum(1 for m in maintenances if m.status == 'مكتمل')
    preventive = sum(1 for m in maintenances if m.type == 'وقائية')
    corrective = sum(1 for m in maintenances if m.type in ['تصحيحية', 'طارئة'])
    total_cost = sum(m.cost for m in maintenances)

    month_names = ['يناير', 'فبراير', 'مارس', 'أبريل', 'مايو', 'يونيو',
                   'يوليو', 'أغسطس', 'سبتمبر', 'أكتوبر', 'نوفمبر', 'ديسمبر']
    month_name = month_names[month - 1]

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">📅 التقرير الشهري</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">{{ month_name }} {{ year }}</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <form method="get" class="d-inline-flex gap-2">
          <select name="month" class="form-select" style="width:auto;">
            {% for i in range(1, 13) %}
            <option value="{{ i }}" {{ 'selected' if i==month }}>
              {{ ['يناير','فبراير','مارس','أبريل','مايو','يونيو','يوليو','أغسطس','سبتمبر','أكتوبر','نوفمبر','ديسمبر'][i-1] }}
            </option>
            {% endfor %}
          </select>
          <input name="year" type="number" value="{{ year }}" class="form-control" style="width:100px;">
          <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">عرض</button>
        </form>
        <a href="{{ url_for('report_monthly_print') }}?year={{ year }}&month={{ month }}" 
           class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan"><p>إجمالي المهام</p><h3>{{ total }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card green"><p>مكتملة</p><h3>{{ completed }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card purple"><p>وقائية</p><h3>{{ preventive }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card red"><p>تصحيحية</p><h3>{{ corrective }}</h3></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-12"><div class="stat-card orange">
        <p>💰 إجمالي التكاليف</p>
        <h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_cost) }} {{ get_setting('currency', 'ر.ي') }}</h3>
      </div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-header">📋 تفاصيل المهام</div>
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>الرقم</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>النوع</th><th>الحالة</th>
          <th>المهندس</th><th>التكلفة</th><th>التاريخ</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for m in maintenances %}
        <tr>
          <td><a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_view', id=m.id) }}">{{ m.number }}</a></td>
          <td>{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.type }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.status }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.engineer.name if m.engineer else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(m.cost) }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="7" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">📅</div>
          <h5>لا توجد بيانات لهذا الشهر</h5>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', year=year, month=month, month_name=month_name,
        maintenances=maintenances, total=total, completed=completed,
        preventive=preventive, corrective=corrective, total_cost=total_cost)

@login_required
def report_monthly_print():
    today = date.today()
    year = request.args.get('year', today.year, type=int)
    month = request.args.get('month', today.month, type=int)
    start = date(year, month, 1)
    end = date(year + 1, 1, 1) - timedelta(days=1) if month == 12 else date(year, month + 1, 1) - timedelta(days=1)
    maintenances = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at >= start,
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at <= datetime.combine(end, datetime.max.time())
    ).all()
    month_names = ['يناير', 'فبراير', 'مارس', 'أبريل', 'مايو', 'يونيو',
                   'يوليو', 'أغسطس', 'سبتمبر', 'أكتوبر', 'نوفمبر', 'ديسمبر']
    month_name = month_names[month - 1]
    total = len(maintenances)
    completed = sum(1 for m in maintenances if m.status == 'مكتمل')
    total_cost = sum(m.cost for m in maintenances)

    return render_template_string('''
    <!DOCTYPE html>

          <td>{{ m.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% endfor %}

    </body>

        total=total, completed=completed, total_cost=total_cost)


# ============================================================
#                     التقرير السنوي
# ============================================================

@app.route('/reports/annual')
@login_required
def report_annual():
    year = request.args.get('year', date.today().year, type=int)
    start = date(year, 1, 1)
    end = date(year, 12, 31)
    maintenances = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at >= start,
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at <= datetime.combine(end, datetime.max.time())
    ).all()
    total = len(maintenances)
    completed = sum(1 for m in maintenances if m.status == 'مكتمل')
    total_cost = sum(m.cost for m in maintenances)

    monthly_data = []
    for m_num in range(1, 13):
        m_start = date(year, m_num, 1)
        m_end = date(year + 1, 1, 1) - timedelta(days=1) if m_num == 12 else date(year, m_num + 1, 1) - timedelta(days=1)
        count = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
            MaintenanceRecord.created_at >= m_start,
            MaintenanceRecord.created_at <= datetime.combine(m_end, datetime.max.time())
        ).count()
        monthly_data.append(count)

    years = sorted(set([m.created_at.year for m in MaintenanceRecord.query.all()]), reverse=True)
    if year not in years:
        years.insert(0, year)

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">📆 التقرير السنوي</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">سنة {{ year }}</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <form method="get" class="d-inline">
          <select name="year" class="form-select d-inline-block" style="width:auto;" onchange="this.form.submit()">
            {% for y in years %}<option value="{{ y }}" {{ 'selected' if y==year }}>{{ y }}</option>{% endfor %}
          </select>
        </form>
        <a href="{{ url_for('report_annual_print') }}?year={{ year }}" class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan"><p>إجمالي المهام</p><h3>{{ total }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card green"><p>مكتملة</p><h3>{{ completed }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card orange"><p>معدل الإنجاز</p>
        <h3>{{ "%.0f"|format((completed / total * 100) if total > 0 else 0) }}%</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card red"><p>التكاليف</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_cost) }}</h3></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-header">📈 أوامر الصيانة على مدار السنة</div>
      <div class="card-body"><canvas id="annualChart" height="80"></canvas></div>
    </div>
    ''', year=year, years=years, total=total, completed=completed,
        total_cost=total_cost, monthly_data=monthly_data) + '''
    <script src="''' + url_for('med_static_v2', filename='js/chart.js') + '''"></script>
    <script>
      new Chart(document.getElementById('annualChart'), {
        type: 'bar',
        data: {
          labels: ['يناير','فبراير','مارس','أبريل','مايو','يونيو','يوليو','أغسطس','سبتمبر','أكتوبر','نوفمبر','ديسمبر'],
          datasets: [{
            label: 'عدد أوامر الصيانة',
            data: ''' + str(monthly_data) + ''',
            backgroundColor: '#1565C0',
            borderRadius: 8
          }]
        },
        options: { responsive: true, plugins: { legend: { display: false } }, scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true } } }
      });
    </script>'''

# ============================================================

@app.route('/reports/preventive')
@login_required
def report_preventive():
    from_date = request.args.get('from', (date.today().replace(day=1)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    to_date = request.args.get('to', date.today().strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    try:
        fd = datetime.strptime(from_date, '%Y-%m-%d')
        td = datetime.strptime(to_date, '%Y-%m-%d').replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59)
    except ValueError:
        fd = datetime.combine(date.today().replace(day=1), datetime.min.time())
        td = datetime.now()
    items = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.type == 'وقائية',
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at.between(fd, td)
    ).order_by(MaintenanceRecord.id.desc()).all()
    total = len(items)
    completed = sum(1 for m in items if m.status == 'مكتمل')
    total_cost = sum(m.cost for m in items)
    total_hours = sum(m.duration_hours for m in items)

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#2E7D32;">🛡️ تقرير الصيانة الوقائية</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">من {{ from_date }} إلى {{ to_date }}</p>
      </div>
      <a href="{{ url_for('report_preventive_print') }}?from={{ from_date }}&to={{ to_date }}" 
         class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-3"><div class="card-body">
      <form class="row g-2">
        <div class="col-md-4"><input type="date" name="from" value="{{ from_date }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><input type="date" name="to" value="{{ to_date }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><button class="btn btn-primary w-100">🔄 تحديث</button></div>
      </form>
    </div></div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan"><p>الإجمالي</p><h3>{{ total }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card green"><p>مكتملة</p><h3>{{ completed }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card orange"><p>الساعات</p><h3>{{ "%.0f"|format(total_hours) }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card purple"><p>التكاليف</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_cost) }}</h3></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>الرقم</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>الوصف</th><th>الحالة</th>
          <th>المهندس</th><th>الساعات</th><th>التكلفة</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for m in items %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ m.number }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.description[:50] if m.description else '-' }}</td>
          <td>
            {% if m.status == 'مكتمل' %}<span class="badge bg-success">مكتمل</span>
            {% elif m.status == 'قيد العمل' %}<span class="badge bg-warning">قيد العمل</span>
            {% else %}<span class="badge bg-secondary">{{ m.status }}</span>{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>{{ m.engineer.name if m.engineer else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.duration_hours }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(m.cost) }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="7" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">🛡️</div><h5>لا توجد بيانات</h5>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', items=items, from_date=from_date, to_date=to_date,
        total=total, completed=completed, total_cost=total_cost, total_hours=total_hours)


@app.route('/reports/preventive/print')
@login_required
def report_preventive_print():
    from_date = request.args.get('from', (date.today().replace(day=1)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    to_date = request.args.get('to', date.today().strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    try:
        fd = datetime.strptime(from_date, '%Y-%m-%d')
        td = datetime.strptime(to_date, '%Y-%m-%d').replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59)
    except ValueError:
        fd = datetime.combine(date.today().replace(day=1), datetime.min.time())
        td = datetime.now()
    items = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.type == 'وقائية',
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at.between(fd, td)
    ).all()
    return render_template_string('''
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="ar" dir="rtl">
    <head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>تقرير الوقائية</title>
      <link rel="stylesheet" href="''' + url_for('med_static_v2', filename='css/bootstrap.rtl.min.css') + '''">
      <style>body { font-family: Tahoma; padding: 20px; }
        .header { text-align: center; border-bottom: 3px double #2E7D32; padding-bottom: 15px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
        @media print { .no-print { display: none !important; } }</style>
    </head>
    <body>
      <div class="no-print text-center mb-3">
        <button onclick="window.print()" class="btn btn-primary">🖨️ طباعة</button>
        <button onclick="window.close()" class="btn btn-secondary">إغلاق</button>
      </div>
      <div class="header">
        <h2>🏥 {{ get_setting('hospital_name', 'المستشفى') }}</h2>
        <p>قسم الهندسة الطبية</p>
        <h3>تقرير الصيانة الوقائية</h3>
        <p>من {{ from_date }} إلى {{ to_date }}</p>
      </div>
      <table class="table table-bordered">
        <tr><td><b>الإجمالي:</b></td><td>{{ items|length }}</td>
            <td><b>التكاليف:</b></td><td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(items|sum(attribute='cost')) }}</td></tr>
      </table>
      <table class="table table-bordered">
        <thead style="background:#2E7D32; color:white;">
          <tr><th>#</th><th>الرقم</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>الحالة</th><th>التكلفة</th></tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for m in items %}
        <tr><td>{{ loop.index }}</td><td>{{ m.number }}</td>
            <td>{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</td>
            <td>{{ m.status }}</td>
            <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(m.cost) }}</td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </body>
    </html>''', items=items, from_date=from_date, to_date=to_date)


# ============================================================
#                     تقرير الصيانة التصحيحية
# ============================================================

@app.route('/reports/corrective')
@login_required
def report_corrective():
    from_date = request.args.get('from', (date.today().replace(day=1)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    to_date = request.args.get('to', date.today().strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    try:
        fd = datetime.strptime(from_date, '%Y-%m-%d')
        td = datetime.strptime(to_date, '%Y-%m-%d').replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59)
    except ValueError:
        fd = datetime.combine(date.today().replace(day=1), datetime.min.time())
        td = datetime.now()
    items = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.type.in_(['تصحيحية', 'طارئة']),
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at.between(fd, td)
    ).order_by(MaintenanceRecord.id.desc()).all()
    total = len(items)
    completed = sum(1 for m in items if m.status == 'مكتمل')
    total_cost = sum(m.cost for m in items)
    total_hours = sum(m.duration_hours for m in items)
    avg_hours = total_hours / total if total > 0 else 0

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#C62828;">🛠️ تقرير الصيانة التصحيحية</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">من {{ from_date }} إلى {{ to_date }}</p>
      </div>
      <a href="{{ url_for('report_corrective_print') }}?from={{ from_date }}&to={{ to_date }}" 
         class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-3"><div class="card-body">
      <form class="row g-2">
        <div class="col-md-4"><input type="date" name="from" value="{{ from_date }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><input type="date" name="to" value="{{ to_date }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><button class="btn btn-primary w-100">🔄 تحديث</button></div>
      </form>
    </div></div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan"><p>الإجمالي</p><h3>{{ total }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card green"><p>مكتملة</p><h3>{{ completed }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card orange"><p>متوسط الساعات</p><h3>{{ "%.1f"|format(avg_hours) }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card red"><p>التكاليف</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_cost) }}</h3></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>الرقم</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>النوع</th><th>الأولوية</th>
          <th>الحالة</th><th>المهندس</th><th>الساعات</th><th>التكلفة</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for m in items %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ m.number }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.type }}</td>
          <td><span class="priority-{{ m.priority }}">{{ m.priority }}</span></td>
          <td>
            {% if m.status == 'مكتمل' %}<span class="badge bg-success">مكتمل</span>
            {% elif m.status == 'قيد العمل' %}<span class="badge bg-warning">قيد العمل</span>
            {% else %}<span class="badge bg-secondary">{{ m.status }}</span>{% endif %}
          </td>
          <td>{{ m.engineer.name if m.engineer else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.duration_hours }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(m.cost) }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="8" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">🛠️</div><h5>لا توجد بيانات</h5>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', items=items, from_date=from_date, to_date=to_date,
        total=total, completed=completed, total_cost=total_cost,
        total_hours=total_hours, avg_hours=avg_hours)

# ============================================================
#                     تقرير أداء المهندسين
# ============================================================

@app.route('/reports/engineers')
@login_required
def report_engineers():
    from_date = request.args.get('from', (date.today().replace(day=1)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    to_date = request.args.get('to', date.today().strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    try:
        fd = datetime.strptime(from_date, '%Y-%m-%d')
        td = datetime.strptime(to_date, '%Y-%m-%d').replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59)
    except ValueError:
        fd = datetime.combine(date.today().replace(day=1), datetime.min.time())
        td = datetime.now()

    engineers_data = []
    for e in Engineer.query.filter_by(active=True).all():
        records = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
            MaintenanceRecord.assigned_to == e.id,
            MaintenanceRecord.created_at.between(fd, td)
        ).all()
        total = len(records)
        completed = sum(1 for m in records if m.status == 'مكتمل')
        hours = sum(m.duration_hours for m in records)
        cost = sum(m.cost for m in records)
        engineers_data.append({
            'engineer': e, 'total': total, 'completed': completed,
            'hours': hours, 'cost': cost,
            'rate': (completed / total * 100) if total > 0 else 0
        })
    engineers_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['completed'], reverse=True)

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">👷 تقرير أداء المهندسين</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">من {{ from_date }} إلى {{ to_date }}</p>
      </div>
      <a href="{{ url_for('report_engineers_print') }}?from={{ from_date }}&to={{ to_date }}" 
         class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-3"><div class="card-body">
      <form class="row g-2">
        <div class="col-md-4"><input type="date" name="from" value="{{ from_date }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><input type="date" name="to" value="{{ to_date }}" class="form-control"></div>
        <div class="col-md-4"><button class="btn btn-primary w-100">🔄 تحديث</button></div>
      </form>
    </div></div>

    <div class="card">
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr>
          <th>#</th><th>المهندس</th><th>التخصص</th><th>الإجمالي</th>
          <th>مكتملة</th><th>معدل الإنجاز</th><th>الساعات</th><th>التكاليف</th>
        </tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for d in engineers_data %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ loop.index }}</td>
          <td><b>{{ d.engineer.name }}</b></td>
          <td>{{ d.engineer.specialty or '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ d.total }}</td>
          <td class="text-success"><b>{{ d.completed }}</b></td>
          <td>
            <div style="background:#E8ECF1; height:8px; border-radius:4px; width:100px;">
              <div style="background: {{ '#2E7D32' if d.rate >= 80 else ('#F9A825' if d.rate >= 50 else '#C62828') }}; 
                          width:{{ d.rate }}%; height:100%; border-radius:4px;"></div>
            </div>
            <small>{{ "%.0f"|format(d.rate) }}%</small>
          </td>
          <td>{{ "%.1f"|format(d.hours) }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(d.cost) }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% else %}
        <tr><td colspan="8" class="empty-state">
          <div class="icon">👷</div><h5>لا توجد بيانات</h5>
        </td></tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    ''', engineers_data=engineers_data, from_date=from_date, to_date=to_date)


@app.route('/reports/engineers/print')
@login_required
def report_engineers_print():
    from_date = request.args.get('from', (date.today().replace(day=1)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    to_date = request.args.get('to', date.today().strftime('%Y-%m-%d'))
    try:
        fd = datetime.strptime(from_date, '%Y-%m-%d')
        td = datetime.strptime(to_date, '%Y-%m-%d').replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59)
    except ValueError:
        fd = datetime.combine(date.today().replace(day=1), datetime.min.time())
        td = datetime.now()
    engineers_data = []
    for e in Engineer.query.filter_by(active=True).all():
        records = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
            MaintenanceRecord.assigned_to == e.id,
            MaintenanceRecord.created_at.between(fd, td)
        ).all()
        total = len(records)
        completed = sum(1 for m in records if m.status == 'مكتمل')
        hours = sum(m.duration_hours for m in records)
        engineers_data.append({
            'engineer': e, 'total': total, 'completed': completed,
            'hours': hours, 'rate': (completed / total * 100) if total > 0 else 0
        })
    engineers_data.sort(key=lambda x: x['completed'], reverse=True)
    return render_template_string('''
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="ar" dir="rtl">
    <head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>أداء المهندسين</title>
      <link rel="stylesheet" href="''' + url_for('med_static_v2', filename='css/bootstrap.rtl.min.css') + '''">
      <style>body { font-family: Tahoma; padding: 20px; }
        .header { text-align: center; border-bottom: 3px double #1565C0; padding-bottom: 15px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
        @media print { .no-print { display: none !important; } }</style>
    </head>
    <body>
      <div class="no-print text-center mb-3">
        <button onclick="window.print()" class="btn btn-primary">🖨️ طباعة</button>
        <button onclick="window.close()" class="btn btn-secondary">إغلاق</button>
      </div>
      <div class="header">
        <h2>🏥 {{ get_setting('hospital_name', 'المستشفى') }}</h2>
        <p>قسم الهندسة الطبية</p>
        <h3>تقرير أداء المهندسين</h3>
        <p>من {{ from_date }} إلى {{ to_date }}</p>
      </div>
      <table class="table table-bordered">
        <thead style="background:#1565C0; color:white;">
          <tr><th>#</th><th>المهندس</th><th>الإجمالي</th><th>مكتملة</th><th>المعدل</th><th>الساعات</th></tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for d in engineers_data %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ loop.index }}</td>
          <td>{{ d.engineer.name }}</td>
          <td>{{ d.total }}</td>
          <td>{{ d.completed }}</td>
          <td>{{ "%.0f"|format(d.rate) }}%</td>
          <td>{{ "%.1f"|format(d.hours) }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </body>
    </html>''', engineers_data=engineers_data, from_date=from_date, to_date=to_date)


# ============================================================
#                     تقرير التكاليف
# ============================================================

@app.route('/reports/costs')
@login_required
def report_costs():
    year = request.args.get('year', date.today().year, type=int)
    start = date(year, 1, 1)
    end = date(year, 12, 31)
    maintenances = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at >= start,
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at <= datetime.combine(end, datetime.max.time())
    ).all()
    total_cost = sum(m.cost for m in maintenances)
    avg_cost = total_cost / len(maintenances) if maintenances else 0

    by_type = {}
    for m in maintenances:
        by_type[m.type] = by_type.get(m.type, 0) + m.cost

    by_department = {}
    for m in maintenances:
        if m.equipment and m.equipment.department:
            name = m.equipment.department.name
            by_department[name] = by_department.get(name, 0) + m.cost

    monthly_costs = []
    for m_num in range(1, 13):
        m_start = date(year, m_num, 1)
        m_end = date(year + 1, 1, 1) - timedelta(days=1) if m_num == 12 else date(year, m_num + 1, 1) - timedelta(days=1)
        cost = db.session.query(func.coalesce(func.sum(MaintenanceRecord.cost), 0)).filter(
            MaintenanceRecord.created_at >= m_start,
            MaintenanceRecord.created_at <= datetime.combine(m_end, datetime.max.time())
        ).scalar()
        monthly_costs.append(float(cost))

    years = sorted(set([m.created_at.year for m in MaintenanceRecord.query.all()]), reverse=True)
    if year not in years:
        years.insert(0, year)

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">💰 تقرير التكاليف</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">سنة {{ year }}</p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <form method="get" class="d-inline">
          <select name="year" class="form-select d-inline-block" style="width:auto;" onchange="this.form.submit()">
            {% for y in years %}<option value="{{ y }}" {{ 'selected' if y==year }}>{{ y }}</option>{% endfor %}
          </select>
        </form>
        <a href="{{ url_for('report_costs_print') }}?year={{ year }}" class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-4"><div class="stat-card cyan"><p>إجمالي التكاليف</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total_cost) }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-4"><div class="stat-card green"><p>متوسط التكلفة</p><h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(avg_cost) }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-4"><div class="stat-card purple"><p>عدد المهام</p><h3>{{ maintenances|length }}</h3></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-4">
      <div class="card-header">📊 التكاليف الشهرية</div>
      <div class="card-body"><canvas id="costsChart" height="80"></canvas></div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3">
      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card h-100">
          <div class="card-header">🔧 التكاليف حسب النوع</div>
          <table class="table mb-0">
            <tbody>
            {% for type, cost in by_type.items() %}
            <tr><td><b>{{ type }}</b></td><td class="text-end">{{ "{:,.0f}".format(cost) }}</td></tr>
            {% else %}
            <tr><td colspan="2" class="text-center text-muted py-3">لا توجد بيانات</td></tr>
            {% endfor %}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card h-100">
          <div class="card-header">🏢 التكاليف حسب القسم</div>
          <table class="table mb-0">
            <tbody>
            {% for dept, cost in by_department.items() %}
            <tr><td><b>{{ dept }}</b></td><td class="text-end">{{ "{:,.0f}".format(cost) }}</td></tr>
            {% else %}
            <tr><td colspan="2" class="text-center text-muted py-3">لا توجد بيانات</td></tr>
            {% endfor %}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    ''', year=year, years=years, total_cost=total_cost, avg_cost=avg_cost,
        maintenances=maintenances, by_type=by_type, by_department=by_department,
        monthly_costs=monthly_costs) + '''
    <script src="''' + url_for('med_static_v2', filename='js/chart.js') + '''"></script>
    <script>
      new Chart(document.getElementById('costsChart'), {
        type: 'bar',
        data: {
          labels: ['يناير','فبراير','مارس','أبريل','مايو','يونيو','يوليو','أغسطس','سبتمبر','أكتوبر','نوفمبر','ديسمبر'],
          datasets: [{
            label: 'التكاليف',
            data: ''' + str(monthly_costs) + ''',
            backgroundColor: '#C62828',
            borderRadius: 8
          }]
        },
        options: { responsive: true, plugins: { legend: { display: false } }, scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true } } }
      });
    </script>'''


@app.route('/reports/costs/print')
@login_required
def report_costs_print():
    year = request.args.get('year', date.today().year, type=int)
    start = date(year, 1, 1)
    end = date(year, 12, 31)
    maintenances = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at >= start,
        MaintenanceRecord.created_at <= datetime.combine(end, datetime.max.time())
    ).all()
    total = sum(m.cost for m in maintenances)

    return render_template_string('''
    <!DOCTYPE html>
    <html lang="ar" dir="rtl">
    <head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>تقرير التكاليف {{ year }}</title>
      <link rel="stylesheet" href="''' + url_for('med_static_v2', filename='css/bootstrap.rtl.min.css') + '''">
      <style>body { font-family: Tahoma; padding: 20px; }
        .header { text-align: center; border-bottom: 3px double #1565C0; padding-bottom: 15px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
        @media print { .no-print { display: none !important; } }</style>
    </head>
    <body>
      <div class="no-print text-center mb-3">
        <button onclick="window.print()" class="btn btn-primary">🖨️ طباعة</button>
        <button onclick="window.close()" class="btn btn-secondary">إغلاق</button>
      </div>
      <div class="header">
        <h2>🏥 {{ get_setting('hospital_name', 'المستشفى') }}</h2>
        <p>قسم الهندسة الطبية</p>
        <h3>تقرير التكاليف - {{ year }}</h3>
        <p>التاريخ: {{ today_date }}</p>
      </div>
      <table class="table table-bordered">
        <tr>
          <td><b>إجمالي التكاليف:</b></td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(total) }} {{ get_setting('currency', 'ر.ي') }}</td>
          <td><b>عدد المهام:</b></td>
          <td>{{ maintenances|length }}</td>
        </tr>
      </table>
      <table class="table table-bordered">
        <thead style="background:#1565C0; color:white;">
          <tr><th>#</th><th>الرقم</th><th>الجهاز</th><th>النوع</th><th>التكلفة</th></tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for m in maintenances %}
        <tr>
          <td>{{ loop.index }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.number }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '-' }}</td>
          <td>{{ m.type }}</td>
          <td>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(m.cost) }}</td>
        </tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </body>
    </html>''', maintenances=maintenances, year=year, total=total,
        today_date=date.today())

@app.route('/reports/equipment-status')
@login_required
def report_equipment_status():
    total = Equipment.query.count()
    working = Equipment.query.filter_by(status='يعمل').count()
    broken = Equipment.query.filter_by(status='معطل').count()
    maintenance = Equipment.query.filter_by(status='صيانة').count()

    by_department = db.session.query(
        Department.name, func.count(Equipment.id)
    ).join(Equipment, Equipment.department_id == Department.id)\
        .group_by(Department.id).all()

    by_category = db.session.query(
        Equipment.category, func.count(Equipment.id)
    ).group_by(Equipment.category).all()

    today = date.today()
    overdue = Equipment.query.filter(
        Equipment.next_maintenance < today,
        Equipment.status != 'مستبعد'
    ).all()

    return render('''
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">🏥 تقرير حالة الأجهزة</h3>
        <p style="margin:0; color:#78909C; font-size:13px;">{{ total }} جهاز</p>
      </div>
      <a href="{{ url_for('report_equipment_status_print') }}" class="btn btn-secondary" target="_blank">🖨️ طباعة</a>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card cyan"><p>الإجمالي</p><h3>{{ total }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card green"><p>يعمل</p><h3>{{ working }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card red"><p>معطل</p><h3>{{ broken }}</h3></div></div>
      <div class="col-md-3"><div class="stat-card orange"><p>صيانة</p><h3>{{ maintenance }}</h3></div></div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card h-100">
          <div class="card-header">🏢 الأجهزة حسب القسم</div>
          <table class="table mb-0">
            <tbody>
            {% for dept, count in by_department %}
            <tr><td><b>{{ dept or 'غير محدد' }}</b></td>
                <td class="text-end"><span class="badge bg-info">{{ count }}</span></td></tr>
            {% endfor %}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card h-100">
          <div class="card-header">📁 الأجهزة حسب الفئة</div>
          <table class="table mb-0">
            <tbody>
            {% for cat, count in by_category %}
            <tr><td><b>{{ cat or 'غير مصنف' }}</b></td>
                <td class="text-end"><span class="badge bg-secondary">{{ count }}</span></td></tr>
            {% endfor %}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    {% if overdue %}
    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-header bg-danger text-white">⚠️ أجهزة تحتاج صيانة ({{ overdue|length }})</div>
      <table class="table mb-0">
        <thead><tr><th>الجهاز</th><th>القسم</th><th>الصيانة المستحقة</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
        {% for e in overdue %}
        <tr>
          <td><a href="{{ url_for('equipment_view', id=e.id) }}"><b>{{ e.name }}</b></a></td>
          <td>{{ e.department.name if e.department else '-' }}</td>
          <td class="text-danger"><b>{{ e.next_maintenance }}</b></td>
        </tr>
        {% endfor %}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    {% endif %}
    ''', total=total, working=working, broken=broken, maintenance=maintenance,
        by_department=by_department, by_category=by_category, overdue=overdue)

@app.route('/export/equipment.csv')
@login_required
def export_equipment_csv():
    si = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(si)
    w.writerow(['الكود', 'الاسم', 'الفئة', 'الشركة', 'الموديل', 'S/N',
                'القسم', 'الموقع', 'الحالة', 'الأهمية'])
    for e in Equipment.query.all():
        w.writerow([
            e.code, e.name, e.category, e.manufacturer, e.model, e.serial_number,
            e.department.name if e.department else '',
            e.location, e.status, e.criticality
        ])
    return Response(si.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), mimetype='text/csv',
                    headers={'Content-Disposition': 'attachment;filename=equipment.csv'})


@app.route('/export/engineers.csv')
@login_required
def export_engineers_csv():
    si = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(si)
    w.writerow(['#', 'الاسم', 'الوظيفة', 'التخصص', 'الهاتف', 'البريد'])
    for e in Engineer.query.all():
        w.writerow([e.id, e.name, e.job_title, e.specialty, e.phone, e.email])
    return Response(si.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), mimetype='text/csv',
                    headers={'Content-Disposition': 'attachment;filename=engineers.csv'})


@app.route('/export/departments.csv')
@login_required
def export_departments_csv():
    si = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(si)
    w.writerow(['#', 'القسم', 'الطابق', 'المبنى', 'المسؤول', 'الهاتف'])
    for d in Department.query.all():
        w.writerow([d.id, d.name, d.floor, d.building,
                    d.manager_name, d.manager_phone])
    return Response(si.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), mimetype='text/csv',
                    headers={'Content-Disposition': 'attachment;filename=departments.csv'})


@app.route('/export/maintenance.csv')
@login_required
def export_maintenance_csv():
    si = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(si)
    w.writerow(['الرقم', 'الجهاز', 'النوع', 'الأولوية', 'الحالة',
                'المهندس', 'التكلفة', 'المدة', 'التاريخ'])
    for m in MaintenanceRecord.query.all():
        w.writerow([
            m.number,
            m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '',
            m.type, m.priority, m.status,
            m.engineer.name if m.engineer else '',
            m.cost, m.duration_hours,
            m.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
        ])
    return Response(si.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), mimetype='text/csv',
                    headers={'Content-Disposition': 'attachment;filename=maintenance.csv'})


@app.route('/export/parts.csv')
@login_required
def export_parts_csv():
    si = io.StringIO()
    w = csv.writer(si)
    w.writerow(['الكود', 'الاسم', 'الفئة', 'الكمية', 'الوحدة',
                'سعر الشراء', 'القيمة', 'حد التنبيه'])
    for p in SparePart.query.all():
        w.writerow([p.code, p.name, p.category, p.quantity, p.unit,
                    p.purchase_price, p.quantity * p.purchase_price, p.alert_limit])
    return Response(si.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), mimetype='text/csv',
                    headers={'Content-Disposition': 'attachment;filename=parts.csv'})
# ============================================================
#                     KPI Dashboard
# ============================================================

@app.route('/kpi')
@login_required
def kpi_dashboard():
    today = date.today()
    total_equipment = Equipment.query.count()
    working = Equipment.query.filter_by(status='يعمل').count()
    availability = (working / total_equipment * 100) if total_equipment > 0 else 0

    repaired = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(
        MaintenanceRecord.status == 'مكتمل',
        MaintenanceRecord.duration_hours > 0
    ).all()
    mttr = sum(m.duration_hours for m in repaired) / len(repaired) if repaired else 0

    preventive = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter_by(type='وقائية').count()
    corrective = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter_by(type='تصحيحية').count()
    preventive_ratio = (preventive / (preventive + corrective) * 100) if (preventive + corrective) > 0 else 0

    total_cost = db.session.query(func.coalesce(func.sum(MaintenanceRecord.cost), 0)).scalar()
    avg_cost = total_cost / len(repaired) if repaired else 0

    critical_equipment = Equipment.query.filter_by(criticality='حرج').count()
    critical_working = Equipment.query.filter_by(criticality='حرج', status='يعمل').count()

    overdue = Equipment.query.filter(
        Equipment.next_maintenance < today,
        Equipment.status != 'مستبعد'
    ).count()

    open_tickets = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='مفتوح').count()
    resolved_tickets = Ticket.query.filter(Ticket.resolution_hours > 0).all()
    avg_resolution = sum(t.resolution_hours for t in resolved_tickets) / len(resolved_tickets) if resolved_tickets else 0

    return render('''
    <h3 class="mb-4" style="color:#1565C0;">📈 مؤشرات الأداء الرئيسية (KPI)</h3>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-4">
        <div class="stat-card {{ 'green' if availability >= 90 else ('orange' if availability >= 70 else 'red') }}">
          <span class="stat-icon">📈</span>
          <p>نسبة التوفر (Availability)</p>
          <h3>{{ "%.1f"|format(availability) }}%</h3>
          <small>هدف: 95%+</small>
          <div style="background:#E8ECF1; height:8px; border-radius:4px; margin-top:8px;">
            <div style="background: {{ '#2E7D32' if availability >= 90 else ('#F9A825' if availability >= 70 else '#C62828') }}; 
                        width:{{ availability }}%; height:100%; border-radius:4px;"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="col-md-4">
        <div class="stat-card cyan">
          <span class="stat-icon">⏱️</span>
          <p>MTTR - متوسط زمن الإصلاح</p>
          <h3>{{ "%.1f"|format(mttr) }} ساعة</h3>
          <small>كلما قل = أفضل</small>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="col-md-4">
        <div class="stat-card {{ 'green' if preventive_ratio >= 70 else 'orange' }}">
          <span class="stat-icon">🛡️</span>
          <p>نسبة الصيانة الوقائية</p>
          <h3>{{ "%.1f"|format(preventive_ratio) }}%</h3>
          <small>هدف: 70%+</small>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="row g-3 mb-4">
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card purple">
          <span class="stat-icon">🚨</span>
          <p>الأجهزة الحرجة</p>
          <h3>{{ critical_equipment }}</h3>
          <small>{{ critical_working }} تعمل</small>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card red">
          <span class="stat-icon">⚠️</span>
          <p>صيانة متأخرة</p>
          <h3>{{ overdue }}</h3>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card orange">
          <span class="stat-icon">💸</span>
          <p>متوسط تكلفة الصيانة</p>
          <h3>{{ "{:,.0f}".format(avg_cost) }}</h3>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="col-md-3">
        <div class="stat-card cyan">
          <span class="stat-icon">🎫</span>
          <p>تذاكر مفتوحة</p>
          <h3>{{ open_tickets }}</h3>
          <small>متوسط الإغلاق: {{ "%.1f"|format(avg_resolution) }} س</small>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">
      <div class="card-header">📊 ملخص المؤشرات</div>
      <div class="card-body p-0">
        <table class="table mb-0">
          <thead><tr>
            <th>المؤشر</th><th>القيمة الحالية</th><th>الحالة</th>
          </tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr>
              <td><b>نسبة توفر الأجهزة</b></td>
              <td>{{ "%.1f"|format(availability) }}%</td>
              <td>
                {% if availability >= 95 %}<span class="badge bg-success">ممتاز</span>
                {% elif availability >= 90 %}<span class="badge bg-info">جيد</span>
                {% elif availability >= 70 %}<span class="badge bg-warning">مقبول</span>
                {% else %}<span class="badge bg-danger">ضعيف</span>{% endif %}
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td><b>نسبة الصيانة الوقائية</b></td>
              <td>{{ "%.1f"|format(preventive_ratio) }}%</td>
              <td>
                {% if preventive_ratio >= 70 %}<span class="badge bg-success">ممتاز</span>
                {% elif preventive_ratio >= 50 %}<span class="badge bg-warning">مقبول</span>
                {% else %}<span class="badge bg-danger">يحتاج تحسين</span>{% endif %}
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td><b>MTTR (متوسط زمن الإصلاح)</b></td>
              <td>{{ "%.1f"|format(mttr) }} ساعة</td>
              <td>
                {% if mttr <= 4 %}<span class="badge bg-success">ممتاز</span>
                {% elif mttr <= 12 %}<span class="badge bg-info">جيد</span>
                {% else %}<span class="badge bg-warning">يحتاج تحسين</span>{% endif %}
              </td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
    ''', total_equipment=total_equipment, working=working,
        availability=availability, mttr=mttr,
        preventive_ratio=preventive_ratio, total_cost=total_cost,
        avg_cost=avg_cost, critical_equipment=critical_equipment,
        critical_working=critical_working, overdue=overdue,
        open_tickets=open_tickets, avg_resolution=avg_resolution)


# ============================================================
#                     التنبيهات
# ============================================================

@app.route('/alerts')
@login_required
def alerts():
    today = date.today()
    future_30 = date.fromordinal(today.toordinal() + 30)

    overdue = Equipment.query.filter(
        Equipment.next_maintenance < today,
        Equipment.status != 'مستبعد'
    ).order_by(Equipment.next_maintenance).all()

    upcoming = Equipment.query.filter(
        Equipment.next_maintenance >= today,
        Equipment.next_maintenance <= future_30,
        Equipment.status != 'مستبعد'
    ).order_by(Equipment.next_maintenance).all()

    broken = Equipment.query.filter_by(status='معطل').order_by(Equipment.criticality).all()

    low_parts = SparePart.query.filter(
        SparePart.quantity <= SparePart.alert_limit
    ).all()

    calibration_due = Calibration.query.filter(
        Calibration.next_calibration <= future_30
    ).order_by(Calibration.next_calibration).all()

    open_tickets = Ticket.query.filter_by(status='مفتوح').order_by(Ticket.priority).all()

    return render('''
    <h3 class="mb-4" style="color:#1565C0;">🔔 التنبيهات الذكية</h3>
    <p style="color:#78909C;">افتح هذه الصفحة كل صباح لمعرفة ما يحتاج انتباهك</p>

# ============================================================

@app.route('/search')
@login_required
def global_search():
    q = request.args.get('q', '').strip()
    if not q:
        return redirect(url_for('dashboard'))

    equipment = Equipment.query.filter(or_(
        Equipment.name.contains(q),
        Equipment.code.contains(q),
        Equipment.serial_number.contains(q),
        Equipment.manufacturer.contains(q)
    )).limit(10).all()

    engineers = Engineer.query.filter(or_(
        Engineer.name.contains(q),
        Engineer.specialty.contains(q)
    )).limit(10).all()

    maintenances = MaintenanceRecord.query.filter(or_(
        MaintenanceRecord.number.contains(q),
        MaintenanceRecord.description.contains(q)
    )).limit(10).all()

    parts = SparePart.query.filter(or_(
        SparePart.name.contains(q),
        SparePart.code.contains(q)
    )).limit(10).all()

    tickets = Ticket.query.filter(or_(
        Ticket.number.contains(q),
        Ticket.title.contains(q)
    )).limit(10).all()

    return render('''
    <h3 class="mb-4">🔍 نتائج البحث عن: "<span style="color:#1565C0;">{{ q }}</span>"</h3>

    <div class="row g-3">
      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">🏥 الأجهزة ({{ equipment|length }})</div>
          <table class="table mb-0"><tbody>
            {% for e in equipment %}
            <tr>
              <td><b>{{ e.name }}</b> <small class="text-muted">{{ e.code or '' }}</small></td>
              <td><a href="{{ url_for('equipment_view', id=e.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-info">عرض</a></td>
            </tr>
            {% else %}
            <tr><td class="text-center text-muted py-3">لا نتائج</td></tr>
            {% endfor %}
          </tbody></table>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">👷 المهندسين ({{ engineers|length }})</div>
          <table class="table mb-0"><tbody>
            {% for e in engineers %}
            <tr>
              <td>{{ e.name }} - {{ e.specialty or '' }}</td>
              <td><a href="{{ url_for('engineer_profile', id=e.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-info">عرض</a></td>
            </tr>
            {% else %}
            <tr><td class="text-center text-muted py-3">لا نتائج</td></tr>
            {% endfor %}
          </tbody></table>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">🔧 أوامر الصيانة ({{ maintenances|length }})</div>
          <table class="table mb-0"><tbody>
            {% for m in maintenances %}
            <tr>
              <td>{{ m.number }} - {{ m.equipment.name if m.equipment else '' }}</td>
              <td><a href="{{ url_for('maintenance_view', id=m.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-info">عرض</a></td>
            </tr>
            {% else %}
            <tr><td class="text-center text-muted py-3">لا نتائج</td></tr>
            {% endfor %}
          </tbody></table>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">📦 قطع الغيار ({{ parts|length }})</div>
          <table class="table mb-0"><tbody>
            {% for p in parts %}
            <tr>
              <td>{{ p.name }} <small class="text-muted">{{ p.code or '' }}</small></td>
              <td><a href="{{ url_for('part_form', id=p.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-info">عرض</a></td>
            </tr>
            {% else %}
            <tr><td class="text-center text-muted py-3">لا نتائج</td></tr>
            {% endfor %}
          </tbody></table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {% if tickets %}
      <div class="col-md-12">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">🎫 التذاكر ({{ tickets|length }})</div>
          <table class="table mb-0"><tbody>
            {% for t in tickets %}
            <tr>
              <td><b>{{ t.number }}</b> - {{ t.title }}</td>
              <td><a href="{{ url_for('ticket_view', id=t.id) }}" class="btn btn-sm btn-info">عرض</a></td>
            </tr>
            {% endfor %}
          </tbody></table>
        </div>
      </div>
      {% endif %}
    </div>
    ''', q=q, equipment=equipment, engineers=engineers,
        maintenances=maintenances, parts=parts, tickets=tickets)

@app.route('/settings', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
@head_required
def settings():
    if request.method == 'POST':
        for k in ['hospital_name', 'department_name', 'hospital_address',
                  'hospital_phone', 'head_name', 'head_phone', 'currency']:
            set_setting(k, request.form.get(k, ''))
        flash('⚙️ تم حفظ الإعدادات', 'success')
        return redirect(url_for('settings'))

    return render('''
    <h3 class="mb-4" style="color:#1565C0;">⚙️ الإعدادات</h3>

    <div class="card mb-4">
      <div class="card-header">🏥 بيانات المستشفى والقسم</div>
      <div class="card-body">
        <form method="post">
          <div class="row g-3">
            <div class="col-md-6"><label>اسم المستشفى</label>
              <input name="hospital_name" value="{{ get_setting('hospital_name', '') }}" class="form-control"></div>
            <div class="col-md-6"><label>اسم القسم</label>
              <input name="department_name" value="{{ get_setting('department_name', 'قسم الهندسة الطبية') }}" class="form-control"></div>
            <div class="col-md-6"><label>هاتف المستشفى</label>
              <input name="hospital_phone" value="{{ get_setting('hospital_phone', '') }}" class="form-control"></div>
            <div class="col-md-6"><label>العملة</label>
              <input name="currency" value="{{ get_setting('currency', 'ر.ي') }}" class="form-control"></div>
            <div class="col-12"><label>العنوان</label>
              <input name="hospital_address" value="{{ get_setting('hospital_address', '') }}" class="form-control"></div>
            <div class="col-md-6"><label>اسم رئيس القسم</label>
              <input name="head_name" value="{{ get_setting('head_name', '') }}" class="form-control"></div>
            <div class="col-md-6"><label>هاتف رئيس القسم</label>

          </div>

            <button class="btn btn-primary btn-lg">💾 حفظ الإعدادات</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card mb-4">
      <div class="card-header">💾 النسخ الاحتياطي</div>
      <div class="card-body">
        <p>احفظ نسخة كاملة من قاعدة البيانات في مكان آمن.</p>
        <a href="{{ url_for('download_backup') }}" class="btn btn-primary">
          📥 تنزيل نسخة احتياطية
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="card">

          <tr><td><b>الإصدار:</b></td><td>2.1</td></tr>
          <tr><td><b>قاعدة البيانات:</b></td><td><code>med_tech_v2.db</code></td></tr>
          <tr><td><b>المنفذ:</b></td><td><code>9000</code></td></tr>
          <tr><td><b>مجلد المرفقات:</b></td><td><code>med_uploads_v2/</code></td></tr>

@app.route('/backup')
@login_required
@head_required
def download_backup():
    db_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, 'med_tech_v2.db')
    if not os.path.exists(db_path):
        flash('❌ قاعدة البيانات غير موجودة', 'danger')
        return redirect(url_for('settings'))
    return send_from_directory(BASE_DIR, 'med_tech_v2.db', as_attachment=True,
                                download_name=f'med_tech_v2_{date.today()}.db')


# ============================================================
#                     الملف الشخصي
# ============================================================

@app.route('/profile', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
@login_required
def profile():
    u = current_user()
    if request.method == 'POST':
        action = request.form.get('action')

        if action == 'change_password':
            old = request.form.get('old_password', '')
            new = request.form.get('new_password', '')
            confirm = request.form.get('confirm_password', '')

            if not check_password_hash(u.password, old):
                flash('❌ كلمة المرور الحالية خاطئة', 'danger')
            elif len(new) < 4:
                flash('❌ كلمة المرور الجديدة قصيرة', 'danger')
            elif new != confirm:
                flash('❌ كلمتا المرور غير متطابقتين', 'danger')
            else:
                u.password = generate_password_hash(new)
                db.session.commit()
                log_activity('change_password', 'تغيير كلمة المرور')
                flash('✅ تم تغيير كلمة المرور', 'success')

        elif action == 'update_info':
            u.full_name = request.form.get('full_name', '').strip()
            u.email = request.form.get('email', '').strip()
            u.phone = request.form.get('phone', '').strip()
            db.session.commit()
            flash('✅ تم التحديث', 'success')

        return redirect(url_for('profile'))

    return render('''
    <h3 class="mb-4" style="color:#1565C0;">👤 الملف الشخصي</h3>

    <div class="row g-3">
      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card mb-3">
          <div class="card-header">🔐 تغيير كلمة المرور</div>
          <div class="card-body">
            <form method="post">
              <input type="hidden" name="action" value="change_password">
              <div class="mb-3"><label>كلمة المرور الحالية</label>
                <input name="old_password" type="password" class="form-control" required></div>
              <div class="mb-3"><label>كلمة المرور الجديدة</label>
                <input name="new_password" type="password" class="form-control" required minlength="4"></div>
              <div class="mb-3"><label>تأكيد كلمة المرور</label>
                <input name="confirm_password" type="password" class="form-control" required minlength="4"></div>
              <button class="btn btn-warning w-100">🔐 تغيير</button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">✏️ تعديل البيانات</div>
          <div class="card-body">
            <form method="post">
              <input type="hidden" name="action" value="update_info">
              <div class="mb-3"><label>الاسم الكامل</label>
                <input name="full_name" value="{{ u.full_name or '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
              <div class="mb-3"><label>البريد الإلكتروني</label>
                <input name="email" type="email" value="{{ u.email or '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
              <div class="mb-3"><label>الهاتف</label>
                <input name="phone" value="{{ u.phone or '' }}" class="form-control"></div>
              <button class="btn btn-primary w-100">💾 حفظ</button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="col-md-6">
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-header">ℹ️ معلومات الحساب</div>
          <div class="card-body">
            <table class="table table-sm mb-0">
              <tr><td><b>اسم المستخدم:</b></td><td>{{ u.username }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>الاسم الكامل:</b></td><td>{{ u.full_name or '-' }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>الصلاحية:</b></td>
                <td>
                  <span class="badge bg-{{ 'danger' if u.role=='admin' else ('warning' if u.role=='head' else 'info') }}">
                    {% if u.role == 'admin' %}مدير عام
                    {% elif u.role == 'head' %}رئيس قسم
                    {% elif u.role == 'engineer' %}مهندس
                    {% elif u.role == 'technician' %}فني
                    {% else %}مشاهد{% endif %}
                  </span>
                </td>
              </tr>
              <tr><td><b>البريد:</b></td><td>{{ u.email or '-' }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>الهاتف:</b></td><td>{{ u.phone or '-' }}</td></tr>
              <tr><td><b>تاريخ الإنشاء:</b></td><td>{{ u.created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d') }}</td></tr>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    ''', u=u)

@app.route('/users')
@login_required
@head_required
def users_list():

    return render('''

      <div>
        <h3 style="margin:0; color:#1565C0;">👥 المستخدمين</h3>
